local fastStrengthFolder = AutoFarm:AddFolder("🔥خاصية الريبر السريع")

local leaderstats = LocalPlayer:WaitForChild("leaderstats")
local rebirthsStat = leaderstats:WaitForChild("الريبر")

local footerالوقتLabel = fastStrengthFolder:AddLabel("0d 0h 0m 0s")
footerالوقتLabel.TextSize = 18
local footerالريبرLabel = fastStrengthFolder:AddLabel("الريبر:✅ 0")
footerالريبرLabel.TextSize = 18
local footerالريبرGainedLabel = fastStrengthFolder:AddLabel("عدد الريبر الي سويته✅: 0")
footerالريبرGainedLabel.TextSize = 18
local footerPaceLabel = fastStrengthFolder:AddLabel("المعدل: -- / ساعة | -- / يوم | -- / أسبوع")
footerPaceLabel.TextSize = 16
local footerAveragePaceLabel = fastStrengthFolder:AddLabel("المتوسط: -- / ساعة | -- / يوم | -- / أسبوع")
footerAveragePaceLabel.TextSize = 16

local startالوقت = tick()
local initialالريبر = rebirthsStat.Value
local lastPaceالوقت = tick()
local lastPaceالريبر = initialالريبر
local averageبدءالوقت = tick()
local averageبدءالريبر = initialالريبر

task.spawn(function()
    while true do
        local elapsed = tick() - startالوقت
        local days = math.floor(elapsed / 86400)
        local hours = math.floor((elapsed % 86400) / 3600)
        local minutes = math.floor((elapsed % 3600) / 60)
        local seconds = math.floor(elapsed % 60)
        footerالوقتLabel.Text = string.format("%dd %dh %dm %ds", days, hours, minutes, seconds)
        task.wait(1)
    end
end)

local function updateالريبر()
    local current = rebirthsStat.Value
    local gained = current - initialالريبر
    footerالريبرLabel.Text = "✅عدد الريبر حقك: " .. current
    footerالريبرGainedLabel.Text = "✅عدد الريبر الي سويته : " .. gained

    local now = tick()
    local gain = current - lastPaceالريبر
    local elapsed = now - lastPaceالوقت
    if gain > 0 and elapsed > 0 then
        local perSecond = gain / elapsed
        footerPaceLabel.Text = string.format(
            "Pace: %.2f / Hour | %.2f / Day | %.2f / Week",
            perSecond * 3600, perSecond * 86400, perSecond * 604800
        )
        lastPaceالريبر = current
        lastPaceالوقت = now
    end

    local averageElapsed = now - averageبدءالوقت
    local averageGain = current - averageبدءالريبر
    if averageGain > 0 and averageElapsed > 0 then
        local averagePerSecond = averageGain / averageElapsed
        footerAveragePaceLabel.Text = string.format(
            "Average: %.2f / Hour | %.2f / Day | %.2f / Week",
            averagePerSecond * 3600, averagePerSecond * 86400, averagePerSecond * 604800
        )
    end
end

rebirthsStat.Changed:Connect(updateالريبر)
updateالريبر()

getgenv().Autoالتدريب = false

local ReplicatedStorage = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
local Players = game:GetService("Players")
local LocalPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer

-- Configuración de mascotas
local FarmPet = "Swift Samurai"
local RebirthPet = "Tribal Overlord"

-- Funciones para equipar/unequipar
local function equipPetByName(name)
    local petsFolder = LocalPlayer:FindFirstChild("petsFolder")
    if not petsFolder then return end
    for _, folder in pairs(petsFolder:GetChildren()) do
        if folder:IsA("Folder") then
            for _, pet in pairs(folder:GetChildren()) do
                if pet.Name == name then
                    ReplicatedStorage.rEvents.equipPetEvent:FireServer("equipPet", pet)
                end
            end
        end
    end
end

local function unequipAllPets()
    local petsFolder = LocalPlayer:FindFirstChild("petsFolder")
    if not petsFolder then return end
    for _, folder in pairs(petsFolder:GetChildren()) do
        if folder:IsA("Folder") then
            for _, pet in pairs(folder:GetChildren()) do
                ReplicatedStorage.rEvents.equipPetEvent:FireServer("unequipPet", pet)
            end
        end
    end
    task.wait(0.1)
end

local function getGoldenRebirthCount()
    local ultimates = LocalPlayer:FindFirstChild("ultimatesFolder")
    if ultimates and ultimates:FindFirstChild("Golden Rebirth") then
        return ultimates["Golden Rebirth"].Value
    end
    return 0
end

local function getStrengthRequiredForRebirth()
    local rebirths = LocalPlayer.leaderstats.الريبر.Value
    local baseStrength = 10000 + (5000 * rebirths)
    local golden = getGoldenRebirthCount()
    if golden >= 1 and golden <= 5 then
        baseStrength = baseStrength * (1 - golden * 0.1)
    end
    return math.floor(baseStrength)
end

-- التدريب parity: automatic best-pet selection for training and rebirth.
local DEVIL_FARM_PETS = {
    ["Omega Overlord"] = {repSpeed=20, strength=0, durability=0, rebirth=0},
    ["Swift Samurai"] = {repSpeed=15, strength=0, durability=0, rebirth=0},
    ["Powercore Hound"] = {repSpeed=0, strength=25, durability=15, rebirth=0},
    ["Titanium Hydra"] = {repSpeed=0, strength=0, durability=0, rebirth=2},
    ["Tribal Overlord"] = {repSpeed=0, strength=0, durability=0, rebirth=2},
    ["Inferno Unicorn"] = {repSpeed=0, strength=25, durability=25, rebirth=0},
    ["Common Boss Pet"] = {repSpeed=5, strength=0, durability=0, rebirth=0},
    ["Rare Boss Pet"] = {repSpeed=7, strength=0, durability=0, rebirth=0},
    ["Epic Boss Pet"] = {repSpeed=10, strength=0, durability=0, rebirth=0},
    ["Rainbow Boss Pet"] = {repSpeed=10, strength=7, durability=0, rebirth=0},
    ["Legendary Boss Pet"] = {repSpeed=10, strength=10, durability=0, rebirth=0},
    ["Mythic Boss Pet"] = {repSpeed=10, strength=20, durability=0, rebirth=0},
    ["Inferno Drake"] = {repSpeed=40, strength=0, durability=0, rebirth=0},
}

local DEVIL_FARM_MAX_PET_SLOTS = 12
local DEVIL_FARM_TARGET_REP_SPEED = 100

local function getDevilOwnedConfiguredPets()
    local petsFolder = LocalPlayer:FindFirstChild("petsFolder")
    local owned = {}
    if not petsFolder then return owned end

    for _, folder in ipairs(petsFolder:GetChildren()) do
        if folder:IsA("Folder") then
            for _, pet in ipairs(folder:GetChildren()) do
                local cfg = DEVIL_FARM_PETS[pet.Name]
                if cfg then
                    table.insert(owned, {
                        instance=pet, name=pet.Name,
                        repSpeed=cfg.repSpeed, strength=cfg.strength,
                        durability=cfg.durability, rebirth=cfg.rebirth
                    })
                end
            end
        end
    end
    return owned
end

local function equipDevilBestالتدريبPets()
    unequipAllPets()
    local pets = getDevilOwnedConfiguredPets()
    table.sort(pets, function(a,b) return a.repSpeed > b.repSpeed end)

    local equipped = {}
    local count = 0
    local repSpeed = 0

    for _, pet in ipairs(pets) do
        if count >= DEVIL_FARM_MAX_PET_SLOTS or repSpeed >= DEVIL_FARM_TARGET_REP_SPEED then break end
        if pet.repSpeed > 0 then
            pcall(function() ReplicatedStorage.rEvents.equipPetEvent:FireServer("equipPet", pet.instance) end)
            equipped[pet.instance] = true
            count += 1
            repSpeed += pet.repSpeed
        end
    end

    table.sort(pets, function(a,b)
        return (a.strength + a.durability) > (b.strength + b.durability)
    end)

    for _, pet in ipairs(pets) do
        if count >= DEVIL_FARM_MAX_PET_SLOTS then break end
        if not equipped[pet.instance] and (pet.strength > 0 or pet.durability > 0) then
            pcall(function() ReplicatedStorage.rEvents.equipPetEvent:FireServer("equipPet", pet.instance) end)
            equipped[pet.instance] = true
            count += 1
        end
    end
end

local function equipDevilBestRebirthPets()
    unequipAllPets()
    local pets = getDevilOwnedConfiguredPets()
    table.sort(pets, function(a,b) return a.rebirth > b.rebirth end)
    local count = 0
    for _, pet in ipairs(pets) do
        if count >= DEVIL_FARM_MAX_PET_SLOTS then break end
        if pet.rebirth > 0 then
            pcall(function() ReplicatedStorage.rEvents.equipPetEvent:FireServer("equipPet", pet.instance) end)
            count += 1
        end
    end
end

-- DEVIL PHASE 6: التدريب التكيفي حسب البنق
-- Uses the existing muscleEvent only; no new remotes are introduced.
local DEVIL_PING_TRAINING = {
    enabled = true,
    minBatch = 1,
    maxBatch = 12,
    currentPing = 0,
    lastPingAt = 0,
}

local function devilGetPingSafe()
    local ok, value = pcall(function()
        return game:GetService("Stats").Network.ServerStatsItem["Data Ping"]:GetValue()
    end)
    return ok and tonumber(value) or 0
end

local function devilAdaptiveBatch(ping)
    -- Conservative batches: higher latency means smaller bursts and longer pauses.
    if ping <= 60 then return 12, 0.035 end
    if ping <= 100 then return 10, 0.045 end
    if ping <= 150 then return 8, 0.060 end
    if ping <= 200 then return 6, 0.080 end
    if ping <= 300 then return 4, 0.110 end
    return 2, 0.160
end

local function devilFireAdaptiveReps(enabled)
    if not enabled then
        for _ = 1, 10 do
            LocalPlayer.muscleEvent:FireServer("rep")
        end
        task.wait()
        return
    end

    local now = os.clock()
    if now - DEVIL_PING_TRAINING.lastPingAt >= 0.5 then
        DEVIL_PING_TRAINING.currentPing = devilGetPingSafe()
        DEVIL_PING_TRAINING.lastPingAt = now
    end

    local batch, pause = devilAdaptiveBatch(DEVIL_PING_TRAINING.currentPing)
    batch = math.clamp(batch, DEVIL_PING_TRAINING.minBatch, DEVIL_PING_TRAINING.maxBatch)

    for _ = 1, batch do
        if not getgenv().Autoالتدريب then break end
        pcall(function()
            LocalPlayer.muscleEvent:FireServer("rep")
        end)
    end
    task.wait(pause)
end

local devilPingFolder = fastStrengthFolder:AddFolder("📶 Ping Training")
devilPingFolder:AddLabel("التدريب التكيفي يستخدم Remote التمرين الموجود.")
local devilPingStatus = devilPingFolder:AddLabel("الوضع: تكيفي | البنق: -- مللي ثانية")
local devilPingSwitch = devilPingFolder:AddSwitch("التدريب التكيفي حسب البنق", function(state)
    DEVIL_PING_TRAINING.enabled = state == true
    devilPingStatus.Text = DEVIL_PING_TRAINING.enabled and "Mode: Adaptive" or "Mode: Fixed"
end)
devilPingSwitch:Set(true)

task.spawn(function()
    while utils.isCurrent() do
        if DEVIL_PING_TRAINING.enabled then
            local ping = devilGetPingSafe()
            DEVIL_PING_TRAINING.currentPing = ping
            DEVIL_PING_TRAINING.lastPingAt = os.clock()
            devilPingStatus.Text = string.format("Mode: Adaptive | Ping: %d ms", math.floor(ping))
        end
        task.wait(1)
    end
end)

-- Switch en la library
getgenv()._DEVIL_FastRebirthToggle = fastStrengthFolder:AddSwitch("🔥 ريبر سريع", function(state)
    getgenv().Autoالتدريب = state

    if state then
        warn("⚡ تم التفعيل")

        task.spawn(function()
            while getgenv().Autoالتدريب do
                local requiredStrength = getStrengthRequiredForRebirth()
                print("Necesario para renacer:", requiredStrength)

                -- Fase de farmeo: أفضل Pets تلقائيًا كما في التدريب.
                equipDevilBestالتدريبPets()

                while LocalPlayer.leaderstats.Strength.Value < requiredStrength and getgenv().Autoالتدريب do
                    devilFireAdaptiveReps(DEVIL_PING_TRAINING.enabled)
                end

                if not getgenv().Autoالتدريب then break end

                -- Fase de renacimiento: أفضل Pets ذات Rebirth multiplier.
                equipDevilBestRebirthPets()

                local oldالريبر = LocalPlayer.leaderstats.الريبر.Value
                repeat
                    ReplicatedStorage.rEvents.rebirthRemote:InvokeServer("rebirthRequest")
                    task.wait(0.1)
                until LocalPlayer.leaderstats.الريبر.Value > oldالريبر or not getgenv().Autoالتدريب

                print("Renacimiento hecho. Reiniciando ciclo.")
            end

            print("🛑 Autoالتدريب DETENIDO")
        end)

    else
        warn("🛑 Autoالتدريب DESACTIVADO")
    end
end)

-- ريبر سريع بدون باكات: يستخدم أكبر عدد ممكن من Swift Samurai،
-- ثم يكمل أماكن التجهيز المتبقية بـ Rare Boss Pet.
getgenv().AutoالتدريبNoPacks = false

local NoPacksFarmPet = "Swift Samurai"
local NoPacksBackupPet = "Rare Boss Pet"
local NoPacksRebirthPet = "Tribal Overlord"
local NoPacksMaxEquipped = 13

local function getPetsByNameForNoPacks(petName)
    local petsFolder = LocalPlayer:FindFirstChild("petsFolder")
    if not petsFolder then return {} end

    local pets = {}
    for _, folder in pairs(petsFolder:GetChildren()) do
        if folder:IsA("Folder") then
            for _, pet in pairs(folder:GetChildren()) do
                if pet.Name == petName then
                    table.insert(pets, pet)
                end
            end
        end
    end
    return pets
end

local function equipNoPacksTrainingPets()
    unequipAllPets()

    local swiftPets = getPetsByNameForNoPacks(NoPacksFarmPet)
    local rareBossPets = getPetsByNameForNoPacks(NoPacksBackupPet)
    local equipped = 0

    -- الأولوية دائمًا لكل Swift Samurai المتاح.
    for _, pet in ipairs(swiftPets) do
        ReplicatedStorage.rEvents.equipPetEvent:FireServer("equipPet", pet)
        equipped += 1
    end

    -- إذا لم نصل لحد التجهيز، نعوض النقص بـ Rare Boss Pet.
    if equipped < NoPacksMaxEquipped then
        for _, pet in ipairs(rareBossPets) do
            if equipped >= NoPacksMaxEquipped then break end
            ReplicatedStorage.rEvents.equipPetEvent:FireServer("equipPet", pet)
            equipped += 1
        end
    end
end

local function equipNoPacksRebirthPets()
    unequipAllPets()
    equipPetByName(NoPacksRebirthPet)
end

getgenv()._DEVIL_FastRebirthNoPacksToggle = fastStrengthFolder:AddSwitch("ريبر سريع بدون باكات", function(state)
    getgenv().AutoالتدريبNoPacks = state

    if state then
        warn("⚡ ريبر سريع بدون باكات تم التفعيل")

        task.spawn(function()
            while getgenv().AutoالتدريبNoPacks do
                local requiredStrength = getStrengthRequiredForRebirth()
                print("Necesario para renacer (No Packs):", requiredStrength)

                -- مرحلة التدريب: Swift Samurai أولًا ثم Rare Boss Pet للتعويض.
                equipNoPacksTrainingPets()

                while LocalPlayer.leaderstats.Strength.Value < requiredStrength and getgenv().AutoالتدريبNoPacks do
                    for _ = 1, 10 do
                        LocalPlayer.muscleEvent:FireServer("rep")
                    end
                    task.wait()
                end

                if not getgenv().AutoالتدريبNoPacks then break end

                -- مرحلة الريبر: نفس بتات الريبر المستخدمة في Fast الريبر الحالي.
                equipNoPacksRebirthPets()

                local oldالريبر = LocalPlayer.leaderstats.الريبر.Value
                repeat
                    ReplicatedStorage.rEvents.rebirthRemote:InvokeServer("rebirthRequest")
                    task.wait(0.1)
                until LocalPlayer.leaderstats.الريبر.Value > oldالريبر or not getgenv().AutoالتدريبNoPacks

                print("Renacimiento hecho. Reiniciando ciclo (No Packs).")
            end

            print("🛑 ريبر سريع بدون باكات متوقف")
        end)
    else
        warn("🛑 ريبر سريع بدون باكات تم إيقافه")
    end
end)
local rebirthFolder = AutoFarm:AddFolder("🔥ريبر بدون باكات")

-- Guardar objetivo de rebirths
local targetRebirthAmount = nil
local farmingTarget = false

-- Input de rebirth objetivo
rebirthFolder:AddTextBox("عدد الريبر المطلوب", function(value)
    local amount = tonumber(value) or 1
    local currentالريبر = leaderstats.الريبر.Value
    if amount >= currentالريبر then
        targetRebirthAmount = amount
    end
end, {clear = false})

-- Toggle para llegar al rebirth objetivo
local targetRebirthToggle = rebirthFolder:AddSwitch("بدء", function(enabled)

    farmingTarget = enabled
    if enabled then
        task.spawn(function()
            while farmingTarget do
                local currentالريبر = leaderstats.الريبر.Value
                local requiredStrength = 10000 + 5000 * currentالريبر

                -- Si ya alcanzó el objetivo, apagar toggle
                if targetRebirthAmount and currentالريبر >= targetRebirthAmount then
                    targetRebirthToggle:Set(false)
                    farmingTarget = false
                    break
                end

                -- Hacer rebirth si ya tienes fuerza suficiente
                if leaderstats.Strength.Value >= requiredStrength then
                    replicatedStorage.rEvents.rebirthRemote:InvokeServer("rebirthRequest")
                    task.wait(0.7)
                else
                    -- Farmear fuerza
                    for i = 1, repSpeed do
                        MuscleEvent:FireServer("rep")
                    end
                end

                task.wait(0.1)
            end
        end)
    end
end)

rebirthFolder:AddSwitch("✅تيليبورت للملك", function(enabled)
    if enabled then
        _G.AutoTeleportKing = true
        local targetPosition = Vector3.new(-8750.1318359375, 124.37654876708984, -5861.2666015625)

        while _G.AutoTeleportKing do
            local player = game.Players.LocalPlayer
            local char = player.Character
            if char then
                local rootPart = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                if rootPart then
                    rootPart.CFrame = CFrame.new(targetPosition)
                    rootPart.Velocity = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
                    rootPart.RotVelocity = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
                end
            end
            task.wait(0.1)
        end
    else
        _G.AutoTeleportKing = false
    end
end)

rebirthFolder:AddSwitch("حجم 2", function(enabled)

        _G.AutoSize2 = true

                "changeSize",

    end
end)




local autoEquipToolsFolder = AutoFarm:AddFolder("🔥ادوات اوتو")

-- Botón para desbloquear el Gamepass الرفع التلقائي
autoEquipToolsFolder:AddButton("Gamepass الرفع التلقائي", function()
    local gamepassFolder = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage").gamepassIds
    local player = game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer
    for _, gamepass in pairs(gamepassFolder:GetChildren()) do
        local value = Instance.new("IntValue")
        value.Name = gamepass.Name
        value.Value = gamepass.Value
        value.Parent = player.ownedGamepasses
    end
end)

-- Función para crear switches de auto-equip
local function createAutoToolSwitch(toolName, globalVar)
    autoEquipToolsFolder:AddSwitch("Auto " .. toolName, function(Value)
        _G[globalVar] = Value
        
        if Value then
            local tool = LocalPlayer.Backpack:FindFirstChild(toolName)
            if tool then
                LocalPlayer.Character.Humanoid:EquipTool(tool)
            end
        else
            local character = LocalPlayer.Character
            local equipped = character:FindFirstChild(toolName)
            if equipped then
                equipped.Parent = LocalPlayer.Backpack
            end
        end
        
        task.spawn(function()
            while _G[globalVar] do
                if not _G[globalVar] then break end
                LocalPlayer.muscleEvent:FireServer("rep")
                task.wait(0.1)
            end
        end)
    end)
end

createAutoToolSwitch("Weight", "AutoWeight")
createAutoToolSwitch("Pushups", "AutoPushups")
createAutoToolSwitch("Handstands", "AutoHandstands")
createAutoToolSwitch("Situps", "AutoSitups")


autoEquipToolsFolder:AddSwitch("لكم تلقائي", function(Value)
    _G.fastHitActive = Value
    
    if Value then
        task.spawn(function()
            while _G.fastHitActive do
                if not _G.fastHitActive then break end
                
                local punch = LocalPlayer.Backpack:FindFirstChild("Punch")
                if punch then
                    punch.Parent = LocalPlayer.Character
                    if punch:FindFirstChild("attackالوقت") then
                        punch.attackالوقت.Value = 0
                    end
                end
                task.wait(0.1)
            end
        end)
        
        task.spawn(function()
            while _G.fastHitActive do
                if not _G.fastHitActive then break end
                
                LocalPlayer.muscleEvent:FireServer("punch", "rightHand")
                LocalPlayer.muscleEvent:FireServer("punch", "leftHand")
                
                local character = LocalPlayer.Character
                if character then
                    local punchTool = character:FindFirstChild("Punch")
                    if punchTool then
                        punchTool:Activate()
                    end
                end
                task.wait()
            end
        end)
    else
        local character = LocalPlayer.Character
        local equipped = character:FindFirstChild("Punch")
        if equipped then
            equipped.Parent = LocalPlayer.Backpack
        end
    end
end)

autoEquipToolsFolder:AddSwitch("أدوات سريعة", function(Value)
    _G.FastTools = Value
    
    local toolSettings = {
        {"Punch", "attackالوقت", Value and 0 or 0.35},
        {"Ground Slam", "attackالوقت", Value and 0 or 6},
        {"Stomp", "attackالوقت", Value and 0 or 7},
        {"Handstands", "repالوقت", Value and 0 or 1},
        {"Pushups", "repالوقت", Value and 0 or 1},
        {"Weight", "repالوقت", Value and 0 or 1},
        {"Situps", "repالوقت", Value and 0 or 1}
    }
    
    local backpack = LocalPlayer:WaitForChild("Backpack")
    
    for _, toolInfo in ipairs(toolSettings) do
        local tool = backpack:FindFirstChild(toolInfo[1])
        if tool and tool:FindFirstChild(toolInfo[2]) then
            tool[toolInfo[2]].Value = toolInfo[3]
        end
        
        local equippedTool = LocalPlayer.Character and LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild(toolInfo[1])
        if equippedTool and equippedTool:FindFirstChild(toolInfo[2]) then
            equippedTool[toolInfo[2]].Value = toolInfo[3]
        end
    end
end)

do
    local BossFight = window:AddTab("جلد الزعماء")

    local Players = game:GetService("Players")
    local RunService = game:GetService("RunService")
    local Workspace = game:GetService("Workspace")
    local TweenService = game:GetService("TweenService")
    local UserInputService = game:GetService("UserInputService")
    local SoundService = game:GetService("SoundService")
    local ReplicatedStorage = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
    local CollectionService = game:GetService("CollectionService")
    local LocalPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer

    local rEvents = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("rEvents")
    local machineRemote = rEvents and rEvents:FindFirstChild("machineInteractRemote")
    local areaTravelRemote = rEvents and rEvents:FindFirstChild("areaTravelRemote")

    -- نفس حالات نظام القتال الأصلي
    local AutoBoss, AutoPunch, AutoChest = false, true, true
    getgenv()._DEVIL_AutoBoss = false
    local AttackDistance, DodgeDistance, FloatHeight = 3, 180, 15
    local Dodging, Connections, LastSequence, ToggleSetters = false, {}, {}, {}
    local BossRenderConn

    -- ربط نظام الحفظ/الاستعادة بزر واحد من قسم جلد الزعماء.
    -- عند إيقافه: Auto Boss يعمل طبيعيًا، لكن نظام حفظ ما كنت تسويه قبل الريبر
    -- (المكان + التدريبات + الاسترجاع بعد البوس) لا يعمل.
    local saveBeforeRebirth = false
    local autoLeaveForBoss = true
    -- حالة التدريب التي كانت موجودة قبل كل رحلة للزعيم.
    -- نحتفظ بكل الحالات التي يمكن لهذا السكربت التحكم بها بدون تغيير منطقها الأصلي.
    local previousStates = {
        RepFarm = false,
        FastRebirth = false,
        FastRebirthNoPacks = false,
        TargetRebirth = false,
        AutoRock = false,
        MainParityRock = false,
        MainParityRockSelected = nil,
        AutoExercise = false,
        AutoExerciseSelected = nil,
    }

    -- Snapshot كامل لكل دورة Boss: المكان الحقيقي + الآلة + الأداة + حالات الفارم.
    -- لا توجد إحداثيات Boulder ثابتة هنا؛ الرجوع يكون إلى آخر مكان فعلي قبل المغادرة.
    local savedBossReturnCFrame = nil
    local savedBossReturnSeat = nil
    local savedBossReturnMachine = nil
    local savedBossReturnToolName = nil
    local savedBossReturnRockName = nil

    local bossStoppedForPrep = false
    local bossWasActuallyFought = false
    local postBossProcessed = false

    local function SetLabel(label, text)
        pcall(function() label:Set(text) end)
        pcall(function() label.Text = text end)
    end

    local timerLabel = BossFight:AddLabel("⏳ الظهور خلال: 00:00")
    local hpLabel = BossFight:AddLabel("👑 صحة الزعيم: جارٍ البحث...")
    local bossFolder = BossFight:AddFolder("🔥 جلد الزعماء")

    bossFolder:AddLabel("حفظ ما كنت تسويه قبل الريبر = يحفظ المكان والتدريب ويرجعهم بعد قتل البوس")
    bossFolder:AddLabel("زعيم تلقائي + لكم تلقائي + فتح صندوق الزعيم تلقائيًا + مراوغة سلسة")

    local SaveBeforeRebirthToggle = bossFolder:AddSwitch("حفظ ما كنت تسويه قبل الريبر", function(state)
        saveBeforeRebirth = state == true

        -- إذا أوقف المستخدم النظام أثناء دورة حفظ حالية، لا نلغي snapshot
        -- الموجود حتى لا نفقد المكان/الحالة التي تم حفظها بالفعل لهذه الدورة.
        if not saveBeforeRebirth then
            -- لا نفعل شيئًا هنا؛ الإيقاف يمنع أي حفظ جديد ويمنع الاسترجاع
            -- للدورات القادمة، بينما الدورة التي بدأت بالفعل تكمل بأمان.
        end
    end)

    local AutoBossToggle = bossFolder:AddSwitch("جمع الزعيم تلقائيًا", function(state)
        AutoBoss = state
        getgenv()._DEVIL_AutoBoss = state
        getgenv()._DEVIL_SetAutoBoss = function(value)
            AutoBoss = value == true
            getgenv()._DEVIL_AutoBoss = AutoBoss
            pcall(function() AutoBossToggle:Set(AutoBoss) end)
            if AutoBoss then setupAllBosses() end
        end
        -- The reference system treats punch/chest collection as part of جمع الزعيم تلقائيًا.
        AutoPunch = state
        AutoChest = state
        if state then
            setupAllBosses()
        end
    end)

    local AutoPunchToggle = bossFolder:AddSwitch("لكم تلقائي", function(state)
        AutoPunch = state
    end)

    local AutoChestToggle = bossFolder:AddSwitch("فتح صندوق الزعيم تلقائيًا", function(state)
        AutoChest = state
    end)

    bossFolder:AddSwitch("خروج تلقائي / استئناف التدريب", function(state)
        autoLeaveForBoss = state
    end)

    local continueAfterBoss = true
    bossFolder:AddSwitch("متابعة التدريب بعد قتل الزعيم", function(state)
        continueAfterBoss = state == true
    end)

    bossFolder:AddLabel("القتال: 3 | المراوغة: 180 | الارتفاع: 15")

    -- اختيار الزعماء: يعتمد أولًا على BossEventConfig ثم يضيف أي Boss مكتشف تلقائيًا.
    local BossChoices = {}
    local BossOrder = {}
    local SelectedBosses = {}
    local BossByDisplayName = {}
    local SelectedBossesLabel = bossFolder:AddLabel("الزعماء المستهدفون: جارٍ الكشف...")
    local BossDropdown = bossFolder:AddDropdown("اختيار زعماء محددين", function(displayName)
        local modelName = BossByDisplayName[displayName]
        if modelName then
            SelectedBosses[modelName] = not SelectedBosses[modelName]
            local names = {}
            for _, name in ipairs(BossOrder) do
                if SelectedBosses[name] then
                    table.insert(names, BossChoices[name] or name)
                end
            end
            SetLabel(SelectedBossesLabel, "Targeted Bosses: " .. (#names > 0 and table.concat(names, ", ") or "None"))
        end
    end)

    local function AddBossChoice(modelName, displayName)
        modelName = tostring(modelName or "")
        displayName = tostring(displayName or modelName)
        if modelName == "" or BossChoices[modelName] then return end
        BossChoices[modelName] = displayName
        BossByDisplayName[displayName] = modelName
        SelectedBosses[modelName] = true
        table.insert(BossOrder, modelName)
        pcall(function() BossDropdown:Add(displayName) end)
        local names = {}
        for _, name in ipairs(BossOrder) do
            if SelectedBosses[name] then table.insert(names, BossChoices[name] or name) end
        end
        SetLabel(SelectedBossesLabel, "Targeted Bosses: " .. (#names > 0 and table.concat(names, ", ") or "None"))
    end

    pcall(function()
        local config = require(ReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild("shared"):WaitForChild("config"):WaitForChild("BossEventConfig"))
        if config and config.RARITIES then
            for _, rarity in ipairs(config.RARITIES) do
                AddBossChoice(rarity.BossModel, rarity.DisplayName or rarity.Name or rarity.BossModel)
            end
        end
    end)

    local alertSound = Instance.new("Sound", SoundService)
    alertSound.Name = "BossالظهورAlertSound"
    alertSound.SoundId = "rbxassetid://9114223178"
    alertSound.Volume = 2.5

    -- نفس SetAllToggles الأصلي: عند الإيقاف لا يتم إجبار Auto Chest على الإيقاف.
    local function SetAllToggles(targetState)
        if AutoBoss ~= targetState then
            pcall(function() AutoBossToggle:Set(targetState) end)
            AutoBoss = targetState
        end
        if AutoPunch ~= targetState then
            pcall(function() AutoPunchToggle:Set(targetState) end)
            AutoPunch = targetState
        end
        if targetState == true then
            if AutoChest ~= true then
                pcall(function() AutoChestToggle:Set(true) end)
                AutoChest = true
            end
        end
    end

    local function GetArena()
        return Workspace:FindFirstChild("Events") and Workspace.Events:FindFirstChild("BossArena")
    end

    -- مطابق للـ GetHealth الأصلي
    local function GetHealth(Model)
        if not Model then return nil, nil, nil end
        local Humanoid = Model:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        if Humanoid then return Humanoid.Health, Humanoid.MaxHealth, Humanoid end

        local CurrentHP, MaxHP
        for Name, Value in pairs(Model:GetAttributes()) do
            local N = tostring(Name):lower()
            if typeof(Value) == "number" then
                if N:find("hp") or N:find("health") then
                    if N:find("max") then MaxHP = Value else CurrentHP = Value end
                end
            end
        end

        for _, Object in ipairs(Model:GetDescendants()) do
            if Object:IsA("NumberValue") or Object:IsA("IntValue") then
                local N = Object.Name:lower()
                if N:find("hp") or N:find("health") then
                    if N:find("max") then MaxHP = Object.Value else CurrentHP = Object.Value end
                end
            end
        end

        return CurrentHP, MaxHP, nil
    end

    -- مطابق للـ FindBoss الأصلي: أعلى Model من حيث HP/MaxHP داخل BossArena.
    local function GetBossHealth(Model)
        if not Model then return nil, nil, nil end
        local stats = Model:FindFirstChild("stats")
        local health = stats and stats:FindFirstChild("Health")
        local maxHealth = stats and stats:FindFirstChild("MaxHealth")
        if health or maxHealth then
            return health and health.Value, maxHealth and maxHealth.Value, nil
        end
        local Humanoid = Model:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        if Humanoid then return Humanoid.Health, Humanoid.MaxHealth, Humanoid end
        local CurrentHP, MaxHP
        for Name, Value in pairs(Model:GetAttributes()) do
            local N = tostring(Name):lower()
            if typeof(Value) == "number" and (N:find("hp") or N:find("health")) then
                if N:find("max") then MaxHP = Value else CurrentHP = Value end
            end
        end
        return CurrentHP, MaxHP, nil
    end

    local function ShouldFarmBoss(model)
        if not model then return false end
        if not BossChoices[model.Name] then
            AddBossChoice(model.Name, Workspace:GetAttribute("BossDisplayName") or model.Name)
        end
        return SelectedBosses[model.Name] == true
    end

    -- البحث الجديد أكثر موثوقية: Tag + BossDamageHitbox + Events/BossEvent/BossArena.
    local function FindBoss()
        local candidates = {}
        for _, model in ipairs(CollectionService:GetTagged("BossEventBoss")) do
            if model:IsA("Model") and model:IsDescendantOf(Workspace) then
                table.insert(candidates, model)
            end
        end
        if #candidates == 0 then
            local events = Workspace:FindFirstChild("Events")
            local folder = events and (events:FindFirstChild("BossEvent") or events:FindFirstChild("BossArena"))
            if folder then
                for _, obj in ipairs(folder:GetDescendants()) do
                    if obj:IsA("Model") and obj:FindFirstChild("BossDamageHitbox", true) then
                        table.insert(candidates, obj)
                    end
                end
            end
        end
        local best, bestHP = nil, 0
        for _, model in ipairs(candidates) do
            local hp, maxHp = GetBossHealth(model)
            local score = maxHp or hp or 0
            if score > bestHP and (hp == nil or hp > 0) and ShouldFarmBoss(model) then
                best, bestHP = model, score
            end
        end
        return best
    end

    local function getBoss(Number)
        local Arena = GetArena()
        if not Arena then return nil end
        local BossName = "Boss" .. Number
        local Boss = Arena:FindFirstChild(BossName)
        if Boss then return Boss end
        for _, obj in ipairs(Arena:GetDescendants()) do
            if obj.Name == BossName and obj:IsA("Model") then return obj end
        end
        return nil
    end

    local function disconnectBossConnections()
        for _, list in pairs(Connections) do
            for _, conn in ipairs(list) do pcall(function() conn:Disconnect() end) end
        end
        Connections, LastSequence = {}, {}
    end

    local function getBossHeadPosition(model, hitbox)
        local head = model and (model:FindFirstChild("Head", true) or model:FindFirstChild("head", true))
        if head and head:IsA("BasePart") then return head.Position end
        if head and head:IsA("Bone") then return head.WorldPosition end
        return hitbox.Position + Vector3.new(0, hitbox.Size.Y * 0.35, 0)
    end

    -- Dodge محدث: Punch خلف الزعيم + Stomp إلى مسافة آمنة، مع توقيت السيرفر.
    local function evadeAttack(model, hitbox, root, humanoid)
        local sequence = model:GetAttribute("BossAttackSequence")
        local kind = string.lower(tostring(model:GetAttribute("BossAttackKind") or ""))
        local attackCFrame = model:GetAttribute("BossAttackCFrame")
        local impactالوقت = model:GetAttribute("BossAttackImpactالوقت")
        if typeof(sequence) ~= "number" or LastSequence[model] == sequence then return nil end
        if typeof(attackCFrame) ~= "CFrame" or typeof(impactالوقت) ~= "number" then return nil end
        LastSequence[model] = sequence
        local now = 0
        pcall(function() now = Workspace:GetServerالوقتNow() end)
        if impactالوقت < now - 0.2 then return nil end

        if kind:find("punch", 1, true) then
            local center = getBossHeadPosition(model, hitbox)
            local dir = Vector3.new(attackCFrame.LookVector.X, 0, attackCFrame.LookVector.Z)
            if dir.Magnitude < 1 then dir = Vector3.new(hitbox.CFrame.LookVector.X, 0, hitbox.CFrame.LookVector.Z) end
            if dir.Magnitude < 1 then dir = Vector3.new(0, 0, 1) end
            local safe = center - dir.Unit * 45
            root.CFrame = CFrame.lookAt(safe, center)
            root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
            root.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
            return "Punch"
        end

        if kind:find("stomp", 1, true) and humanoid then
            if humanoid.Health / math.max(humanoid.MaxHealth, 1) <= 0.35 then
                local dir = Vector3.new(root.Position.X - attackCFrame.Position.X, 0, root.Position.Z - attackCFrame.Position.Z)
                if dir.Magnitude < 1 then dir = Vector3.new(-attackCFrame.LookVector.X, 0, -attackCFrame.LookVector.Z) end
                if dir.Magnitude < 1 then dir = Vector3.new(1, 0, 0) end
                local safe = attackCFrame.Position + dir.Unit * DodgeDistance + Vector3.new(0, FloatHeight + 3, 0)
                root.CFrame = CFrame.lookAt(safe, attackCFrame.Position)
                root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
                root.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
                return "Stomp"
            end
        end
        return nil
    end

    local function setupBossEvents(boss, number)
        if not boss then return end
        if Connections[number] then
            for _, conn in ipairs(Connections[number]) do pcall(function() conn:Disconnect() end) end
        end
        Connections[number] = {}
        local function checkAttack()
            if not AutoBoss then return end
            local kind = tostring(boss:GetAttribute("BossAttackKind") or ""):lower()
            if kind:find("punch", 1, true) or kind:find("stomp", 1, true) then
                -- التنفيذ الفعلي يتم داخل حلقة الهجوم حتى لا تتعارض الـconnections مع respawn.
            end
        end
        table.insert(Connections[number], boss:GetAttributeChangedSignal("BossAttackSequence"):Connect(checkAttack))
        table.insert(Connections[number], boss:GetAttributeChangedSignal("BossAttackKind"):Connect(checkAttack))
    end

    function setupAllBosses()
        disconnectBossConnections()
        for num = 1, 5 do
            local boss = getBoss(num)
            if boss then setupBossEvents(boss, num) end
        end
        for _, boss in ipairs(CollectionService:GetTagged("BossEventBoss")) do
            if boss:IsA("Model") and boss:IsDescendantOf(Workspace) then
                setupBossEvents(boss, boss.Name)
            end
        end
    end

    local function TryClaimPrompt(prompt)
        if not AutoChest or not prompt:IsA("ProximityPrompt") then return end
        local act = string.lower(tostring(prompt.ActionText or ""))
        local obj = string.lower(tostring(prompt.ObjectText or ""))
        if act:find("claim", 1, true) or obj:find("chest", 1, true) or prompt.Name == "bossChestPrompt" then
            pcall(function() fireproximityprompt(prompt) end)
        end
    end

    local function FindBossChest()
        for _, chest in ipairs(CollectionService:GetTagged("BossEventChest")) do
            if chest:IsDescendantOf(Workspace) then
                local prompt = chest:FindFirstChild("bossChestPrompt", true)
                local part = prompt and prompt.Parent or (chest:IsA("Model") and (chest.PrimaryPart or chest:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart", true))) or chest
                if part and part:IsA("BasePart") then return chest, prompt, part end
            end
        end
        local chest = Workspace:FindFirstChild("BossChest", true)
        if chest then
            local prompt = chest:FindFirstChild("bossChestPrompt", true)
            local part = prompt and prompt.Parent or (chest:IsA("Model") and (chest.PrimaryPart or chest:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart", true))) or chest
            if part and part:IsA("BasePart") then return chest, prompt, part end
        end
    end

    Workspace.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(obj)
        if obj:IsA("ProximityPrompt") then
            task.defer(function() TryClaimPrompt(obj) end)
        end
    end)

    task.spawn(function()
        while task.wait(0.5) do
            if AutoChest then
                for _, obj in ipairs(Workspace:GetDescendants()) do TryClaimPrompt(obj) end
            end
        end
    end)

    -- لكم تلقائي محدث: يرسل ضربات اليدين + تفعيل الأداة، مع معدل آمن.
    task.spawn(function()
        while task.wait(0.05) do
            if AutoPunch then
                local char = LocalPlayer.Character
                local hum = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                local backpack = LocalPlayer:FindFirstChild("Backpack")
                if char and hum and hum.Health > 0 then
                    local punch = char:FindFirstChild("Punch") or (backpack and backpack:FindFirstChild("Punch"))
                    if punch then
                        if punch.Parent ~= char then pcall(function() hum:EquipTool(punch) end) end
                        pcall(function()
                            LocalPlayer.muscleEvent:FireServer("punch", "leftHand")
                            LocalPlayer.muscleEvent:FireServer("punch", "rightHand")
                            punch:Activate()
                        end)
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end)

    -- الهجوم + الـdodge الجديدان يعملان على البوس المحدد فقط.
    task.spawn(function()
        while task.wait(0.03) do
            if AutoBoss then
                local boss = FindBoss()
                local char = LocalPlayer.Character
                local root = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                local hum = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                local hitbox = boss and boss:FindFirstChild("BossDamageHitbox", true)
                if boss and root and hum and hum.Health > 0 and hitbox and hitbox:IsA("BasePart") then
                    local evade = evadeAttack(boss, hitbox, root, hum)
                    if evade ~= "Stomp" then
                        local center = getBossHeadPosition(boss, hitbox)
                        if evade ~= "Punch" then
                            root.CFrame = CFrame.lookAt(center, center + hitbox.CFrame.LookVector)
                        end
                        root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
                        root.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
                        local left = char:FindFirstChild("LeftHand")
                        local right = char:FindFirstChild("RightHand")
                        pcall(function()
                            if left then firetouchinterest(hitbox, left, 0); firetouchinterest(hitbox, left, 1) end
                            if right then firetouchinterest(hitbox, right, 0); firetouchinterest(hitbox, right, 1) end
                            LocalPlayer.muscleEvent:FireServer("punch", "leftHand")
                            LocalPlayer.muscleEvent:FireServer("punch", "rightHand")
                        end)
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end)

    -- عرض البوس والـHP والـالظهور باستخدام Attributes الجديدة إن وُجدت.
    task.spawn(function()
        while true do
            local Boss = FindBoss()
            if Boss then
                local HP, MaxHP = GetBossHealth(Boss)
                SetLabel(hpLabel, "👑 " .. (Workspace:GetAttribute("BossDisplayName") or BossChoices[Boss.Name] or Boss.Name) ..
                    "\n❤️ " .. tostring(math.floor(HP or 0)) .. " / " .. tostring(math.floor(MaxHP or 0)))
            else
                SetLabel(hpLabel, "❤️ SEARCHING BOSS...")
            end
            task.wait(0.25)
        end
    end)

    if BossRenderConn then BossRenderConn:Disconnect() end
    BossRenderConn = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
        if not AutoBoss then return end
        local boss = FindBoss()
        if boss then
            local hp, maxHp = GetBossHealth(boss)
            if hp and hp <= 0 then SetAllToggles(false) end
        end
    end)

    -- حفظ/إيقاف/استعادة تدريب DEVIL فقط، مع نفس نافذة 15 ثانية في السكربت الأصلي.
    local function stopAndExitMachine()
        if machineRemote then
            pcall(function() machineRemote:InvokeServer("leaveMachine") end)
        end
    end

    -- لم يعد Auto Boss يعود إلى Boulder ثابت. هذه الدالة أصبحت مجرد احتياط
    -- للحفاظ على توافق باقي السكربت؛ عملية الرجوع الفعلية تستخدم الـ Snapshot المحفوظ.
    local function autoTravelAndEquipBoulder()
        return false
    end

    local function captureTrainingState()
        previousStates.RepFarm = getgenv()._AutoRepFarmEnabled == true
        previousStates.RepFarm250 = getgenv()._DEVIL_RepFarm250Enabled == true
        previousStates.RepFarm150 = getgenv()._DEVIL_RepFarm150Enabled == true
        previousStates.FastRebirth = getgenv().Autoالتدريب == true
        previousStates.FastRebirthNoPacks = getgenv().AutoالتدريبNoPacks == true
        previousStates.TargetRebirth = farmingTarget == true
        previousStates.AutoRock = getgenv().autoFarm == true

        local parityRock = getgenv()._DEVIL_MainParityRockState
        if parityRock then
            previousStates.MainParityRock = parityRock.running == true
            previousStates.MainParityRockSelected = parityRock.selected
        end

        local autoExercise = getgenv()._DEVIL_AutoExerciseState
        if autoExercise then
            previousStates.AutoExercise = autoExercise.running == true
            previousStates.AutoExerciseSelected = autoExercise.selected
        end

        -- احفظ حالة تمارين Squat/Lift أيضًا. هذه الأنظمة لها متغيرات running
        -- محلية داخل addMachineFarm، لذلك يتم تسجيلها في سجل مشترك قبل البوس.
        local machineFarmState = getgenv()._DEVIL_MachineFarmState
        if machineFarmState then
            previousStates.Squat = machineFarmState.Squat and machineFarmState.Squat.running == true
            previousStates.Lift = machineFarmState.Lift and machineFarmState.Lift.running == true
            previousStates.SquatSelected = machineFarmState.Squat and machineFarmState.Squat.selected
            previousStates.LiftSelected = machineFarmState.Lift and machineFarmState.Lift.selected
        else
            previousStates.Squat = false
            previousStates.Lift = false
        end

        savedBossReturnRockName = selectrock

        -- احفظ الأداة التي كان اللاعب يستخدمها، مثل Dumbbells/Pushups أو أي Tool آخر.
        savedBossReturnToolName = nil
        local char = LocalPlayer.Character
        local hum = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        if char then
            for _, obj in ipairs(char:GetChildren()) do
                if obj:IsA("Tool") then
                    savedBossReturnToolName = obj.Name
                    break
                end
            end
        end
    end

    local function stopTraining()
        -- أوقف Squat/Lift مؤقتًا أيضًا، لكن لا نفقد حالتهما لأن Snapshot حفظها.
        local machineFarmState = getgenv()._DEVIL_MachineFarmState
        if machineFarmState then
            if machineFarmState.Squat then machineFarmState.Squat.running = false end
            if machineFarmState.Lift then machineFarmState.Lift.running = false end
        end

        getgenv()._AutoRepFarmEnabled = false
        getgenv()._DEVIL_RepFarm250Enabled = false
        getgenv()._DEVIL_RepFarm150Enabled = false
        getgenv().Autoالتدريب = false
        getgenv().AutoالتدريبNoPacks = false
        getgenv().autoFarm = false

        local parityRock = getgenv()._DEVIL_MainParityRockState
        if parityRock then parityRock.running = false end

        local autoExercise = getgenv()._DEVIL_AutoExerciseState
        if autoExercise then autoExercise.running = false end

        if getgenv()._DEVIL_RepFarmToggle then pcall(function() getgenv()._DEVIL_RepFarmToggle:Set(false) end) end
        if getgenv()._DEVIL_RepFarm150Toggle then pcall(function() getgenv()._DEVIL_RepFarm150Toggle:Set(false) end) end
        if getgenv()._DEVIL_FastRebirthToggle then pcall(function() getgenv()._DEVIL_FastRebirthToggle:Set(false) end) end
        if getgenv()._DEVIL_FastRebirthNoPacksToggle then pcall(function() getgenv()._DEVIL_FastRebirthNoPacksToggle:Set(false) end) end
        if farmingTarget then
            farmingTarget = false
            if getgenv()._DEVIL_TargetRebirthToggle then pcall(function() getgenv()._DEVIL_TargetRebirthToggle:Set(false) end) end
        end
    end

    local function resumeTraining()
        -- نفس الحالات السابقة، بدون تشغيل أي تدريب لم يكن مفعّلًا.
        if previousStates.RepFarm250 and getgenv()._DEVIL_RepFarmToggle then
            pcall(function() getgenv()._DEVIL_RepFarmToggle:Set(true) end)
        end
        if previousStates.RepFarm150 and getgenv()._DEVIL_RepFarm150Toggle then
            pcall(function() getgenv()._DEVIL_RepFarm150Toggle:Set(true) end)
        end
        if previousStates.RepFarm
            and not previousStates.RepFarm250
            and not previousStates.RepFarm150 then
            getgenv()._DEVIL_RepFarm250Enabled = true
            getgenv()._AutoRepFarmEnabled = true
            if getgenv()._DEVIL_RefreshRepFarm then
                pcall(getgenv()._DEVIL_RefreshRepFarm)
            end
        end
        if previousStates.FastRebirth and getgenv()._DEVIL_FastRebirthToggle then
            pcall(function() getgenv()._DEVIL_FastRebirthToggle:Set(true) end)
        elseif previousStates.FastRebirth then
            getgenv().Autoالتدريب = true
        end
        if previousStates.FastRebirthNoPacks and getgenv()._DEVIL_FastRebirthNoPacksToggle then
            pcall(function() getgenv()._DEVIL_FastRebirthNoPacksToggle:Set(true) end)
        elseif previousStates.FastRebirthNoPacks then
            getgenv().AutoالتدريبNoPacks = true
        end
        if previousStates.TargetRebirth and getgenv()._DEVIL_TargetRebirthToggle then
            pcall(function() getgenv()._DEVIL_TargetRebirthToggle:Set(true) end)
        elseif previousStates.TargetRebirth then
            farmingTarget = true
        end
        if previousStates.AutoRock then
            if savedBossReturnRockName ~= nil then selectrock = savedBossReturnRockName end
            getgenv().autoFarm = true
        end

        local parityRock = getgenv()._DEVIL_MainParityRockState
        if parityRock and previousStates.MainParityRock then
            if previousStates.MainParityRockSelected ~= nil then
                parityRock.selected = previousStates.MainParityRockSelected
            end
            parityRock.running = true
        end

        local autoExercise = getgenv()._DEVIL_AutoExerciseState
        if autoExercise and previousStates.AutoExercise then
            if previousStates.AutoExerciseSelected ~= nil then
                autoExercise.selected = previousStates.AutoExerciseSelected
            end
            autoExercise.running = true
        end

        -- أعد Squat/Lift فقط إذا كانا يعملان قبل البوس، وبنفس الاختيار.
        local machineFarmState = getgenv()._DEVIL_MachineFarmState
        if machineFarmState then
            if machineFarmState.Squat and previousStates.Squat then
                if previousStates.SquatSelected ~= nil then
                    machineFarmState.Squat.selected = previousStates.SquatSelected
                end
                if machineFarmState.Squat.toggle then
                    pcall(function() machineFarmState.Squat.toggle:Set(true) end)
                else
                    machineFarmState.Squat.running = true
                end
            end
            if machineFarmState.Lift and previousStates.Lift then
                if previousStates.LiftSelected ~= nil then
                    machineFarmState.Lift.selected = previousStates.LiftSelected
                end
                if machineFarmState.Lift.toggle then
                    pcall(function() machineFarmState.Lift.toggle:Set(true) end)
                else
                    machineFarmState.Lift.running = true
                end
            end
        end

        -- أعد الأداة التي كانت مجهزة قبل رحلة البوس.
        if savedBossReturnToolName then
            task.spawn(function()
                task.wait(0.25)
                local char = LocalPlayer.Character
                local hum = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                local backpack = LocalPlayer:FindFirstChild("Backpack")
                local tool = char and char:FindFirstChild(savedBossReturnToolName) or (backpack and backpack:FindFirstChild(savedBossReturnToolName))
                if tool and hum then pcall(function() hum:EquipTool(tool) end) end
            end)
        end
    end

    local function captureBossReturnState()
        local char = LocalPlayer.Character
        local root = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        local humanoid = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")

        -- أهم نقطة: هذا هو آخر CFrame حقيقي قبل أن يتحرك Auto Boss.
        savedBossReturnCFrame = root and root.CFrame or nil
        savedBossReturnSeat = humanoid and humanoid.SeatPart or nil
        savedBossReturnMachine = nil

        if savedBossReturnSeat then
            local machine = savedBossReturnSeat
            while machine and machine.Parent and machine ~= Workspace do
                if machine.Parent == Workspace:FindFirstChild("machinesFolder") then
                    savedBossReturnMachine = machine
                    break
                end
                machine = machine.Parent
            end
        end
    end

    local function restoreBossReturnState()
        local savedCFrame = savedBossReturnCFrame
        if not savedCFrame then return end

        task.wait(0.15)

        local char = LocalPlayer.Character or Workspace:FindFirstChild(LocalPlayer.Name)
        local root = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if not root then return end

        -- رجوع مباشر إلى نفس المكان، مهما كان نوع المكان داخل الماب.
        pcall(function() root.CFrame = savedCFrame end)
        task.wait(0.2)

        -- إذا كان اللاعب يستخدم آلة، أعد استخدام نفس الـ Seat/آلة.
        local seat = savedBossReturnSeat
        local machine = savedBossReturnMachine
        if seat and seat.Parent and machineRemote then
            pcall(function() machineRemote:InvokeServer("useMachine", seat) end)
        elseif machine and machine:FindFirstChild("interactSeat") and machineRemote then
            pcall(function() machineRemote:InvokeServer("useMachine", machine.interactSeat) end)
        end

        task.wait(0.2)
        if root.Parent then
            pcall(function() root.CFrame = savedCFrame end)
        end
    end

    local function prepareForBoss()
        if bossStoppedForPrep or not autoLeaveForBoss then return end

        -- هذا هو المفتاح الرئيسي للنظام:
        -- بدون تفعيله، لا يتم حفظ المكان/التدريب ولا يتم إيقاف أو استرجاع ما كان يعمل.
        if not saveBeforeRebirth then
            return
        end

        bossStoppedForPrep = true
        bossWasActuallyFought = false
        postBossProcessed = false

        -- احفظ آخر مكان + الآلة + الأداة + حالات الفارم قبل أي حركة إلى البوس.
        captureBossReturnState()
        captureTrainingState()

        stopAndExitMachine()
        stopTraining()
    end

    local function processBossEnd()
        if not bossStoppedForPrep or postBossProcessed then return end

        -- لا نسترجع شيئًا إلا إذا كانت دورة الحفظ قد بدأت من الزر.
        if not saveBeforeRebirth then
            bossStoppedForPrep = false
            bossWasActuallyFought = false
            return
        end

        postBossProcessed = true
        task.spawn(function()
            task.wait(3)

            -- أوقف القتال أولًا، ثم ارجع إلى آخر مكان محفوظ قبل البوس.
            SetAllToggles(false)
            restoreBossReturnState()
            if continueAfterBoss then
                resumeTraining()
            end

            -- إعادة تثبيت حالات الأنظمة المشتركة بعد الرجوع للمكان،
            -- لأن بعض حلقات التدريب قد تعتمد على الشخصية الجديدة/المكان الجديد.
            local parityRock = getgenv()._DEVIL_MainParityRockState
            if parityRock and previousStates.MainParityRock then
                parityRock.running = true
            end
            local autoExercise = getgenv()._DEVIL_AutoExerciseState
            if autoExercise and previousStates.AutoExercise then
                autoExercise.running = true
            end

            bossStoppedForPrep = false
            bossWasActuallyFought = false
            savedBossReturnCFrame = nil
            savedBossReturnSeat = nil
            savedBossReturnMachine = nil
            savedBossReturnToolName = nil
            savedBossReturnRockName = nil
        end)
    end

    -- نفس إعادة الربط بعد Respawn الموجودة في السكربت الأصلي.
    LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(function()
        task.wait(1)
        if AutoBoss then setupAllBosses() end
    end)

    -- نفس دورة السكربت المرجعي: الاعتماد على وقت البوس الذي يرسله السيرفر،
    -- مع اعتبار ظهور البوس الفعلي بداية القتال، واختفائه نهاية الدورة.
    local hasPlayedSound = false
    RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
        local nextالوقت = Workspace:GetAttribute("BossالظهورNextالوقت")
        local secondsLeft = typeof(nextالوقت) == "number" and math.max(0, nextالوقت - Workspace:GetServerالوقتNow()) or 0

        local displayHours = math.floor(secondsLeft / 3600)
        local displayMinutes = math.floor((secondsLeft % 3600) / 60)
        local displaySeconds = math.floor(secondsLeft % 60)

        if secondsLeft > 0 then
            if displayHours > 0 then
                SetLabel(timerLabel, string.format("⏳ %02d:%02d:%02d", displayHours, displayMinutes, displaySeconds))
            else
                SetLabel(timerLabel, string.format("⏳ %02d:%02d", displayMinutes, displaySeconds))
            end
        else
            SetLabel(timerLabel, "🚨 BOSS SPAWNING NOW!")
            if not hasPlayedSound then
                pcall(function() alertSound:Play() end)
                hasPlayedSound = true
            end
        end

        local liveBoss = FindBoss()
        if liveBoss and autoLeaveForBoss then
            if not bossStoppedForPrep then
                prepareForBoss()
            end
            bossWasActuallyFought = bossStoppedForPrep
        elseif not liveBoss then
            hasPlayedSound = false
            if bossStoppedForPrep and bossWasActuallyFought then
                processBossEnd()
            end
        end
    end)
end

getgenv()._DEVIL_TargetRebirthToggle = targetRebirthToggle

local function FormatNumberWithCommas(number)
    local formatted = tostring(number):reverse():gsub("(%d%d%d)", "%1,"):reverse()
    return formatted:gsub("^,", "")
end

local function FormatAbbreviated(number)
    local abbreviations = {"", "K", "M", "B", "T", "Qa", "Qi"}
    local abbreviationIndex = 1
    while number >= 1000 do
        number = number / 1000
        abbreviationIndex = abbreviationIndex + 1
    end
    return string.format("%.2f", number) .. abbreviations[abbreviationIndex]
end

local function FormatDisplay(value)
    local normal = FormatNumberWithCommas(value)
    local abbreviated = FormatAbbreviated(value)
    return "[ " .. normal .. " | " .. abbreviated .. " ]"
end

local enemyHealthLabel = estadisticas:AddLabel("صحة الخصم: غير متاح")
enemyHealthLabel.TextSize = 20

local playerDamageLabel = estadisticas:AddLabel("ضررك: غير متاح")
playerDamageLabel.TextSize = 20
playerDamageLabel.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)

local hitsToKillLabel = estadisticas:AddLabel("عدد الضربات للقتل: غير متاح")
hitsToKillLabel.TextSize = 20
hitsToKillLabel.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)

local function calculateEnemyHealth(targetPlayer)
    if not targetPlayer then
        return 0
    end
    local baseDura = 0
    local durabilityStat =
        targetPlayer:FindFirstChild("Durability") or
        (targetPlayer:FindFirstChild("leaderstats") and targetPlayer.leaderstats:FindFirstChild("Durability"))
    if durabilityStat then
        baseDura = durabilityStat.Value
    end
    local totalMultiplier = 1
    local ultFolder = targetPlayer:FindFirstChild("ultimatesFolder")
    if ultFolder then
        local infernalHealth = ultFolder:FindFirstChild("Infernal Health")
        if infernalHealth then
            local upgrades = infernalHealth.Value or 0
            totalMultiplier = totalMultiplier + 0.15 * upgrades
        end
    end
    local backpack = targetPlayer:FindFirstChild("Backpack")
    if backpack then
        local equippedPets = backpack:FindFirstChild("EquippedPets") or backpack:FindFirstChild("equippedPets")
        if equippedPets then
            for _, pet in ipairs(equippedPets:GetChildren()) do
                if string.lower(tostring(pet)):match("mighty") and string.lower(tostring(pet)):match("monster") then
                    totalMultiplier = totalMultiplier + 0.5
                    break
                end
            end
        end
    end
    local totalHealth = baseDura * totalMultiplier
    return totalHealth
end

local function calculateLocalPlayerDamage()
    local strengthStat = nil
    local leaderstats = Player:FindFirstChild("leaderstats")
    if leaderstats then
        strengthStat = leaderstats:FindFirstChild("Strength")
    end
    if not strengthStat then
        return 0
    end
    local baseDamage = strengthStat.Value * 0.0667
    local totalMultiplier = 1
    local ultFolder = Player:FindFirstChild("ultimatesFolder")
    if ultFolder then
        local demonDamage = ultFolder:FindFirstChild("Demon Damage")
        if demonDamage then
            local upgrades = demonDamage.Value or 0
            totalMultiplier = totalMultiplier + math.min(0.1 * upgrades, 0.5)
        end
    end
    local backpack = Player:FindFirstChild("Backpack")
    if backpack then
        local equippedPets = backpack:FindFirstChild("EquippedPets") or backpack:FindFirstChild("equippedPets")
        if equippedPets then
            for _, pet in ipairs(equippedPets:GetChildren()) do
                if string.lower(tostring(pet)):match("wild") and string.lower(tostring(pet)):match("wizard") then
                    totalMultiplier = totalMultiplier + 0.5
                    break
                end
            end
        end
    end
    baseDamage = baseDamage * totalMultiplier
    return baseDamage
end

local function calculateHitsToKill(health, damage)
    if damage <= 0 then
        return "∞"
    end
    local hits = math.ceil(health / damage)
    if hits > 50 then
        return "∞"
    elseif hits < 1 then
        return 1
    else
        return hits
    end
end

local function updateAdvancedStats(targetPlayer)
    if not targetPlayer then
        enemyHealthLabel.Text = "vida del enemigo: N/A"
        playerDamageLabel.Text = "Tu daño: N/A"
        hitsToKillLabel.Text = "عدد الضربات للقتل: غير متاح"
        return
    end
    local enemyHealth = calculateEnemyHealth(targetPlayer)
    local playerDamage = calculateLocalPlayerDamage()
    local hitsToKill = calculateHitsToKill(enemyHealth, playerDamage)
    enemyHealthLabel.Text = "life of the enemy: " .. FormatDisplay(enemyHealth)
    playerDamageLabel.Text = "You damage: " .. FormatDisplay(playerDamage)
    hitsToKillLabel.Text = "Golpes para matarlo: " .. tostring(hitsToKill)
end

task.spawn(function()
    while true do
        local selectedPlayer = game.Players:FindFirstChild(SelectPlayerName)
        if selectedPlayer then
            updateAdvancedStats(selectedPlayer)
        else
            updateAdvancedStats(nil)
        end
        task.wait(0.2)
    end
end)

local Calculadora = window:AddTab("calculator", Color3.fromRGB(200, 100, 100))

local baseStrength = 0
local resultadoLabelsDamage = {}

local FolderDamage = Calculadora:AddFolder("Pack Damage Calculator")

FolderDamage:AddTextBox("القوة الأساسية (مثال: 1.27Qa, T, B)", function(text)
    local unidades = { ["T"] = 1e12, ["Q"] = 1e15, ["B"] = 1e9 }
    text = text:upper()
    for u, m in pairs(unidades) do
        if text:find(u) then
            local num = tonumber(text:match("(%d+%.?%d*)"))
            if num then
                baseStrength = num * m
                return
            end
        end
    end
    baseStrength = tonumber(text:match("(%d+%.?%d*)")) or 0
end)

local mensajeLabelDamage = FolderDamage:AddLabel("")

for i = 1, 8 do
    resultadoLabelsDamage[i] = FolderDamage:AddLabel(string.format("%d pack(s): -", i))
end

FolderDamage:AddButton("حساب الضرر", function()
    if baseStrength <= 0 then
        mensajeLabelDamage.Text = "Enter a valid value."
        for i = 1, 8 do
            resultadoLabelsDamage[i].Text = string.format("%d pack(s): -", i)
        end
        return
    end

    mensajeLabelDamage.Text = ""

    local danoAjustado = baseStrength * 0.10
    local incremento = 0.335

    for pack = 1, 8 do
        local mult = 1 + (pack * incremento)
        local valor = danoAjustado * mult

        local disp
        if valor >= 1e15 then
            disp = string.format("%.3f Qa", valor / 1e15)
        elseif valor >= 1e12 then
            disp = string.format("%.2f T", valor / 1e12)
        elseif valor >= 1e9 then
            disp = string.format("%.2f B", valor / 1e9)
        else
            disp = tostring(math.floor(valor))
        end

        resultadoLabelsDamage[pack].Text = string.format("%d pack(s): %s", pack, disp)
    end
end)

local baseDurabilidad = 0
local resultadoLabelsDurabilidad = {}

local FolderDurabilidad = Calculadora:AddFolder("Pack Durability Calculator")

FolderDurabilidad:AddTextBox("التحمل الأساسي (مثال: 1.27Qa, T, B)", function(text)
    local unidades = { ["T"] = 1e12, ["Q"] = 1e15, ["B"] = 1e9 }
    text = text:upper()
    for u, m in pairs(unidades) do
        if text:find(u) then
            local num = tonumber(text:match("(%d+%.?%d*)"))
            if num then
                baseDurabilidad = num * m
                return
            end
        end
    end
    baseDurabilidad = tonumber(text:match("(%d+%.?%d*)")) or 0
end)

local mensajeLabelDurabilidad = FolderDurabilidad:AddLabel("")

for i = 1, 8 do
    resultadoLabelsDurabilidad[i] = FolderDurabilidad:AddLabel(string.format("%d pack(s): -", i))
end

FolderDurabilidad:AddButton("حساب التحمل", function()
    if baseDurabilidad <= 0 then
        mensajeLabelDurabilidad.Text = "Enter a valid value."
        for i = 1, 8 do
            resultadoLabelsDurabilidad[i].Text = string.format("%d pack(s): -", i)
        end
        return
    end

    mensajeLabelDurabilidad.Text = ""

    local incremento = 0.335
    local adicional = 1.5

    for pack = 1, 8 do
        local mult = 1 + (pack * incremento)
        local valor = baseDurabilidad * mult * adicional

        local disp
        if valor >= 1e15 then
            disp = string.format("%.3f Qa", valor / 1e15)
        elseif valor >= 1e12 then
            disp = string.format("%.2f T", valor / 1e12)
        elseif valor >= 1e9 then
            disp = string.format("%.2f B", valor / 1e9)
        else
            disp = tostring(math.floor(valor))
        end

        resultadoLabelsDurabilidad[pack].Text = string.format("%d pack(s): %s", pack, disp)
    end
end)

local features = window:AddTab("Calculate Stats")
features:Show()

local player = game.Players.LocalPlayer
local leaderstats = player:WaitForChild("leaderstats")
local strengthStat = leaderstats:WaitForChild("Strength")
local durabilityStat = player:WaitForChild("Durability")

local function formatNumber(number)
    local isNegative = number < 0
    number = math.abs(number)
    if number >= 1e15 then
        return (isNegative and "-" or "") .. string.format("%.2fQa", number / 1e15)
    elseif number >= 1e12 then
        return (isNegative and "-" or "") .. string.format("%.2fT", number / 1e12)
    elseif number >= 1e9 then
        return (isNegative and "-" or "") .. string.format("%.2fB", number / 1e9)
    elseif number >= 1e6 then
        return (isNegative and "-" or "") .. string.format("%.2fM", number / 1e6)
    elseif number >= 1e3 then
        return (isNegative and "-" or "") .. string.format("%.2fK", number / 1e3)
    else
        return (isNegative and "-" or "") .. string.format("%.2f", number)
    end
end

local stopwatchLabel = features:AddLabel("وقت التمرين السريع: 0ي 0س 0د 0ث")
stopwatchLabel.TextSize = 20

local projectedStrengthLabel = features:AddLabel("معدل القوة: 0 /ساعة | 0 /يوم | 0 /أسبوع | 0 /شهر")
projectedStrengthLabel.TextSize = 20

local projectedDurabilityLabel = features:AddLabel("معدل التحمل: 0 /ساعة | 0 /يوم | 0 /أسبوع | 0 /شهر")
projectedDurabilityLabel.TextSize = 20

features:AddLabel("").TextSize = 10

local statsLabel = features:AddLabel("الإحصائيات:")
statsLabel.TextSize = 24

local strengthLabel = features:AddLabel("القوة: 0 | المكتسب: 0")
strengthLabel.TextSize = 20

local durabilityLabel = features:AddLabel("التحمل: 0 | المكتسب: 0")
durabilityLabel.TextSize = 20

local startالوقت = tick()
local initialStrength = strengthStat.Value
local initialDurability = durabilityStat.Value
local trackingبدءed = false

local strengthHistory = {}
local durabilityHistory = {}
local calculationInterval = 10

task.spawn(function()
    local lastCalcالوقت = tick()
    while true do
        local currentالوقت = tick()
        local currentStrength = strengthStat.Value
        local currentDurability = durabilityStat.Value

        if not trackingبدءed and (currentStrength - initialStrength) >= 100e9 then
            trackingبدءed = true
            startالوقت = tick()
            strengthHistory = {}
            durabilityHistory = {}
        end

        if trackingبدءed then
            local elapsedالوقت = currentالوقت - startالوقت
            local days = math.floor(elapsedالوقت / (24 * 3600))
            local hours = math.floor((elapsedالوقت % (24 * 3600)) / 3600)
            local minutes = math.floor((elapsedالوقت % 3600) / 60)
            local seconds = math.floor(elapsedالوقت % 60)

            stopwatchLabel.Text = string.format("Fast Rep الوقت: %dd %dh %dm %ds", days, hours, minutes, seconds)

            local sessionStrengthDelta = currentStrength - initialStrength
            local sessionDurabilityDelta = currentDurability - initialDurability

            strengthLabel.Text = "Strength: " .. formatNumber(currentStrength) .. " | Gained: " .. formatNumber(sessionStrengthDelta)
            durabilityLabel.Text = "Durability: " .. formatNumber(currentDurability) .. " | Gained: " .. formatNumber(sessionDurabilityDelta)

            table.insert(strengthHistory, {time = currentالوقت, value = currentStrength})
            table.insert(durabilityHistory, {time = currentالوقت, value = currentDurability})

            while #strengthHistory > 0 and currentالوقت - strengthHistory[1].time > calculationInterval do
                table.remove(strengthHistory, 1)
            end
            while #durabilityHistory > 0 and currentالوقت - durabilityHistory[1].time > calculationInterval do
                table.remove(durabilityHistory, 1)
            end

            if currentالوقت - lastCalcالوقت >= calculationInterval then
                lastCalcالوقت = currentالوقت

                if #strengthHistory >= 2 then
                    local strengthDelta = strengthHistory[#strengthHistory].value - strengthHistory[1].value
                    local strengthPerSecond = strengthDelta / calculationInterval
                    local strengthPerHour = math.floor(strengthPerSecond * 3600)
                    local strengthPerDay = math.floor(strengthPerSecond * 86400)
                    local strengthPerWeek = math.floor(strengthPerSecond * 604800)
                    local strengthPerMonth = math.floor(strengthPerSecond * 2592000)

                    projectedStrengthLabel.Text = "Strength Rate: " .. formatNumber(strengthPerHour) .. "/Hour | " .. formatNumber(strengthPerDay) .. "/Day | " .. formatNumber(strengthPerWeek) .. "/Week | " .. formatNumber(strengthPerMonth) .. "/Month"
                end

                if #durabilityHistory >= 2 then
                    local durabilityDelta = durabilityHistory[#durabilityHistory].value - durabilityHistory[1].value
                    local durabilityPerSecond = durabilityDelta / calculationInterval
                    local durabilityPerHour = math.floor(durabilityPerSecond * 3600)
                    local durabilityPerDay = math.floor(durabilityPerSecond * 86400)
                    local durabilityPerWeek = math.floor(durabilityPerSecond * 604800)
                    local durabilityPerMonth = math.floor(durabilityPerSecond * 2592000)

                    projectedDurabilityLabel.Text = "Durability Rate: " .. formatNumber(durabilityPerHour) .. "/Hour | " .. formatNumber(durabilityPerDay) .. "/Day | " .. formatNumber(durabilityPerWeek) .. "/Week | " .. formatNumber(durabilityPerMonth) .. "/Month"
                end
            end
        end

        task.wait(0.05)
    end
end)

-- ==================== DEVIL HUB INVENTORY REPLACEMENT ====================
local function __DEVIL_SETUP_INVENTORY()

-- Added from DEVIL HUB V1.01 Inventory without modifying the original DEVIL HUB code.
local InventoryServices = {
    Players = game:GetService("Players"),
    ReplicatedStorage = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage"),
    VirtualUser = game:GetService("VirtualUser"),
    Workspace = workspace,
    Lighting = game:GetService("Lighting"),
    UserInputService = game:GetService("UserInputService"),
    RunService = game:GetService("RunService")
}

local InventoryPlayerData = {
    Player = InventoryServices.Players.LocalPlayer,
    Character = InventoryServices.Players.LocalPlayer.Character,
    Backpack = InventoryServices.Players.LocalPlayer:WaitForChild("Backpack")
}

local InventoryRemotes = {
    MuscleEvent = InventoryPlayerData.Player:WaitForChild("muscleEvent")
}

local Inventory = window:AddTab("Pets")
local Boosts = window:AddTab("Boosts")

-- ============================================================
-- BOOSTS
-- ============================================================
local BoostServices = InventoryServices
local BoostPlayerData = InventoryPlayerData
local BoostRemotes = InventoryRemotes

Boosts:AddLabel("🍫 أكل المعززات:")
local EggEaterData = {Running = false, Generation = 0}
task.spawn(function()
    while true do
        if not Boosts then break end
        if EggEaterData.Running then
            local character = BoostPlayerData.Player.Character
            local tool = character and character:FindFirstChild("Protein Egg") or BoostPlayerData.Backpack:FindFirstChild("Protein Egg")
            if tool then pcall(function() BoostPlayerData.Player.muscleEvent:FireServer("proteinEgg", tool) end) end
        end
        task.wait(0.1)
    end
end)
Boosts:AddSwitch("أكل كل البيض", function(state)
    EggEaterData.Running = state == true
    EggEaterData.Generation += 1
end)

local BoostData = {Running = false, Generation = 0}
Boosts:AddSwitch("أكل كل المعززات", function(state)
    BoostData.Running = state == true
    BoostData.Generation += 1
end)

task.spawn(function()
    while true do
        if BoostData.Running then
            local generation = BoostData.Generation
            local character = BoostPlayerData.Player.Character
            local backpack = BoostPlayerData.Backpack
            local boostItems = {
                {name="Tropical Shake", action="tropicalShake"},
                {name="Energy Shake", action="energyShake"},
                {name="Protein Bar", action="proteinBar"},
                {name="TOUGH Bar", action="toughBar"},
                {name="Protein Shake", action="proteinShake"},
                {name="ULTRA Shake", action="ultraShake"},
                {name="Energy Bar", action="energyBar"},
            }
            local exerciseItems = {
                {name="Protein Egg", action="proteinEgg"},
                {name="Weightlifting Simulator", action="weightliftingSimulator"},
                {name="Pushup", action="pushup"},
                {name="Situp", action="situp"},
            }
            for _, entry in ipairs(boostItems) do
                local itemName, action = entry.name, entry.action
                if not BoostData.Running or BoostData.Generation ~= generation then break end
                local tool = character and character:FindFirstChild(itemName) or backpack:FindFirstChild(itemName)
                if tool then
                    local parts = {}
                    for word in itemName:gmatch("%S+") do parts[#parts+1] = word:lower() end
                    for i=2,#parts do parts[i] = parts[i]:sub(1,1):upper()..parts[i]:sub(2) end
                    pcall(function()
                        for _=1,3 do
                            if not BoostData.Running or BoostData.Generation ~= generation then break end
                            BoostPlayerData.Player.muscleEvent:FireServer(action, tool)
                        end
                    end)
                end
            end

            -- Keep the original exercise/egg eating behavior as part of the same existing switch.
            for _, entry in ipairs(exerciseItems) do
                if not BoostData.Running or BoostData.Generation ~= generation then break end
                local itemName, action = entry.name, entry.action
                local tool = character and character:FindFirstChild(itemName) or backpack:FindFirstChild(itemName)
                if tool then
                    pcall(function()
                        BoostPlayerData.Player.muscleEvent:FireServer(action, tool)
                    end)
                end
            end
        end
        task.wait(0.15)
    end
end)

Boosts:AddLabel("الهدايا:")
