Inventory:AddLabel("🛒 متجر الحيوانات:")

local PetShopData = {PetList={}, Selected={}, SelectedList={}, BuyAmount=1}
local AuraData = {AuraList={}, Selected={}, SelectedList={}, BuyAmount=1}
local EvolveData = {Selected={}, SelectedList={}, Amount=1}

local function getPetShopFolder()
    local shared = Services.ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("shared")
    local runtime = shared and shared:FindFirstChild("runtime")
    return runtime and runtime:FindFirstChild("cPetShopFolder") or Services.ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("cPetShopFolder")
end

local function refreshPetLists()
    table.clear(PetShopData.PetList); table.clear(AuraData.AuraList)
    local folder = getPetShopFolder(); if not folder then return end
    for _, item in ipairs(folder:GetChildren()) do
        if item:GetAttribute("IsPowerUp") == true then table.insert(AuraData.AuraList,item.Name) else table.insert(PetShopData.PetList,item.Name) end
    end
    table.sort(PetShopData.PetList); table.sort(AuraData.AuraList)
end
refreshPetLists()

local function toggleSelected(data,name)
    if not name or name == "" then return end
    if data.Selected[name] then
        data.Selected[name]=nil
        for i,v in ipairs(data.SelectedList) do if v==name then table.remove(data.SelectedList,i); break end end
    else data.Selected[name]=true; table.insert(data.SelectedList,name) end
end

local petLabel=Inventory:AddLabel("الحيوانات المختارة: لا يوجد")
local petDropdown=Inventory:AddDropdown("اختيار الحيوانات",function(text)
    toggleSelected(PetShopData,text); petLabel.Text="Selected Pets: "..(#PetShopData.SelectedList>0 and table.concat(PetShopData.SelectedList,", ") or "None")
end)
for _,name in ipairs(PetShopData.PetList) do petDropdown:Add(name) end
Inventory:AddTextBox("كمية الشراء:",function(text) PetShopData.BuyAmount=math.max(1,math.floor(tonumber(text) or 1)) end,{clear=false})

local function buyPet(name)
    local folder=getPetShopFolder(); local remote=Services.ReplicatedStorage.rEvents:FindFirstChild("cPetShopRemote")
    local obj=folder and folder:FindFirstChild(name); if not remote or not obj then return false end
    return pcall(function() remote:InvokeServer(obj) end)
end

Inventory:AddSwitch("شراء الحيوانات",function(bool)
    _G.AutoHatchPet=bool
    if bool then task.spawn(function()
        while _G.AutoHatchPet do
            for _,name in ipairs(PetShopData.SelectedList) do
                local obj=getPetShopFolder() and getPetShopFolder():FindFirstChild(name)
                if obj then for _=1,PetShopData.BuyAmount do task.spawn(function(p) pcall(function() Services.ReplicatedStorage.rEvents.cPetShopRemote:InvokeServer(p) end) end,obj) end end
            end
            task.wait(0.1)
        end
    end) end
end)

Inventory:AddSwitch("تطوير تلقائي",function(state)

    _G.DEVILAutoEvolve=true

Inventory:AddLabel("🔁 تداول تلقائي:")
local TradeData = {SelectedPlayers={}, SelectedPlayerList={}, SelectedPets={}, SelectedPetList={}, Generation=0}
local tradePlayerLabel = Inventory:AddLabel("اللاعبون: لا يوجد")
local tradePlayerDropdown = Inventory:AddDropdown("اختيار اللاعبين", function(text)
    toggleSelected(TradeData, text)
    tradePlayerLabel.Text = "Players: " .. (#TradeData.SelectedPlayerList > 0 and table.concat(TradeData.SelectedPlayerList, ", ") or "None")
end)
for _, player in ipairs(Services.Players:GetPlayers()) do
    if player ~= Services.Players.LocalPlayer then tradePlayerDropdown:Add(player.DisplayName .. " | " .. player.Name) end
end
Services.Players.PlayerAdded:Connect(function(player)
    if player ~= Services.Players.LocalPlayer then tradePlayerDropdown:Add(player.DisplayName .. " | " .. player.Name) end
end)
Services.Players.PlayerRemoving:Connect(function(player)
    tradePlayerDropdown:Remove(player.DisplayName .. " | " .. player.Name)
    local key = player.Name
    TradeData.SelectedPlayers[key] = nil
    for i,v in ipairs(TradeData.SelectedPlayerList) do if v == key then table.remove(TradeData.SelectedPlayerList,i); break end end
end)

Inventory:AddButton("➕ إضافة جميع اللاعبين", function()
    table.clear(TradeData.SelectedPlayers); table.clear(TradeData.SelectedPlayerList)
    for _, player in ipairs(Services.Players:GetPlayers()) do
        if player ~= Services.Players.LocalPlayer then
            TradeData.SelectedPlayers[player.Name] = true
            TradeData.SelectedPlayerList[#TradeData.SelectedPlayerList+1] = player.Name
        end
    end
    tradePlayerLabel.Text = "Players: All"
end)

local tradePetLabel = Inventory:AddLabel("الحيوانات: لا يوجد")
local tradePetDropdown = Inventory:AddDropdown("اختيار الحيوانات", function(text)
    toggleSelected(TradeData, text)
    tradePetLabel.Text = "Pets: " .. (#TradeData.SelectedPetList > 0 and table.concat(TradeData.SelectedPetList, ", ") or "None")
end)
for _, name in ipairs(PetShopData.PetList) do tradePetDropdown:Add(name) end

local tradeRunning=false
Inventory:AddSwitch("تداول تلقائي", function(state)
    tradeRunning = state == true
    TradeData.Generation += 1
    local generation = TradeData.Generation
    if not tradeRunning then return end
    task.spawn(function()
        while tradeRunning and TradeData.Generation == generation do
            local unique = Services.Players.LocalPlayer:FindFirstChild("petsFolder") and Services.Players.LocalPlayer.petsFolder:FindFirstChild("Unique")
            local tradingEvent = Services.ReplicatedStorage.rEvents:FindFirstChild("tradingEvent")
            if unique and tradingEvent and #TradeData.SelectedPlayerList > 0 and #TradeData.SelectedPetList > 0 then
                for _, username in ipairs(TradeData.SelectedPlayerList) do
                    if not tradeRunning or TradeData.Generation ~= generation then break end
                    local target = Services.Players:FindFirstChild(username)
                    if target then
                        pcall(function() tradingEvent:FireServer("sendTradeRequest", target) end)
                        task.wait(0.5)
                        local offered=0
                        for _, selectedName in ipairs(TradeData.SelectedPetList) do
                            for _, pet in ipairs(unique:GetChildren()) do
                                if not tradeRunning or TradeData.Generation ~= generation or offered >= 6 then break end
                                if pet.Name == selectedName then
                                    pcall(function() tradingEvent:FireServer("offerItem", pet) end)
                                    offered += 1
                                    task.wait(0.03)
                                end
                            end
                            if offered >= 6 then break end
                        end
                        if tradeRunning and TradeData.Generation == generation then
                            pcall(function() tradingEvent:FireServer("acceptTrade") end)
                        end
                        task.wait(0.2)
                    end
                end
            end
            task.wait(1)
        end
    end)
end)

				Services.Players.LocalPlayer.consumablesFolder:FindFirstChild("Tropical Shake"))

task.spawn(function()
	while true do
		local proteinEggCount = 0
		local tropicalShakeCount = 0
		if PlayerData.Backpack then
			for _, item in ipairs(PlayerData.Backpack:GetChildren()) do
				if item.Name == "Protein Egg" then proteinEggCount = proteinEggCount + 1
				elseif item.Name == "Tropical Shake" then tropicalShakeCount = tropicalShakeCount + 1 end
			end
		end
		EggGifterData.ProteinEggLabel.Text = "Protein Eggs: " .. proteinEggCount
		ShakeGifterData.TropicalShakeLabel.Text = "Tropical Shakes: " .. tropicalShakeCount
		task.wait(7.5)
	end
end)

Boosts:AddLabel("قد يتعارض مع المعززات التي استلمتها كهدية. استخدم جهازًا لتقليل اللاق!").TextSize = 14


end

local __inventory_ok, __inventory_err = pcall(__DEVIL_SETUP_INVENTORY)
if not __inventory_ok then
    warn('[DEVIL HUB] Boosts failed to initialize: '..tostring(__inventory_err))
end


local farmTab = window:AddTab("Rock")
farmTab:AddLabel("تدريب الصخور")
 
local function gettool()
    for i, v in pairs(game.Players.LocalPlayer.Backpack:GetChildren()) do
        if v.Name == "Punch" and game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("Humanoid") then
            game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character.Humanoid:EquipTool(v)
        end
    end
    game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer.muscleEvent:FireServer("punch", "leftHand")
    game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer.muscleEvent:FireServer("punch", "rightHand")
end
 
local tinyIslandRockSwitch = farmTab:AddSwitch("صخرة الجزيرة الصغيرة", function(bool)
    selectrock = "صخرة الجزيرة الصغيرة"
    getgenv().autoFarm = bool
 
    if bool then
        spawn(function()
            while getgenv().autoFarm do
                task.wait()
                if game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer.Durability.Value >= 0 then
                    for i, v in pairs(game:GetService("Workspace").machinesFolder:GetDescendants()) do
                        if v.Name == "neededDurability" and v.Value == 0 and 
                           game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("LeftHand") and 
                           game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("RightHand") then
 
                            firetouchinterest(v.Parent.Rock, game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer.Character.RightHand, 0)
                            firetouchinterest(v.Parent.Rock, game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer.Character.RightHand, 1)
                            firetouchinterest(v.Parent.Rock, game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer.Character.LeftHand, 0)
                            firetouchinterest(v.Parent.Rock, game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer.Character.LeftHand, 1)
                            gettool()
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end)
    end
end)

------------------------------------------------------------------
-- DEVIL DASHBOARD + PERFORMANCE TOOLS
------------------------------------------------------------------
do
    local DashboardTab = window:AddTab("DEVIL DASHBOARD")
    local PerformanceTab = window:AddTab("DEVIL SETTINGS")
    local Players = game:GetService("Players")

    -- DEVIL runtime task/connection guards
    getgenv()._DEVIL_TaskLocks = getgenv()._DEVIL_TaskLocks or {}
    getgenv()._DEVIL_Connections = getgenv()._DEVIL_Connections or {}

    getgenv()._DEVIL_RunOnce = function(name)
        if getgenv()._DEVIL_TaskLocks[name] then return false end
        getgenv()._DEVIL_TaskLocks[name] = true
        return true
    end

    getgenv()._DEVIL_ReleaseTask = function(name)
        getgenv()._DEVIL_TaskLocks[name] = nil
    end

    getgenv()._DEVIL_StopAll = function()
        getgenv().Autoالتدريب = false
        getgenv().AutoالتدريبNoPacks = false
        getgenv().AutoHatchPet = false
        getgenv()._DEVIL_MasterMode = false
        getgenv()._DEVIL_MasterState = false
        getgenv()._DEVIL_MasterBossWanted = false
        getgenv()._DEVIL_MasterRebirthWanted = false
        getgenv()._DEVIL_ConfigAutoSave = false
        getgenv()._DEVIL_AutoBoss = false
        getgenv()._DEVIL_TaskLocks = {}

        if type(getgenv()._DEVIL_SetAutoBoss) == "function" then
            pcall(getgenv()._DEVIL_SetAutoBoss, false)
        end

        for key, connection in pairs(getgenv()._DEVIL_Connections) do
            pcall(function()
                if connection and connection.Disconnect then connection:Disconnect() end
            end)
            getgenv()._DEVIL_Connections[key] = nil
        end
    end

    local RunService = game:GetService("RunService")
    local LP = Players.LocalPlayer

    DashboardTab:AddLabel("لوحة تحكم DEVIL HUB")
    DashboardTab:AddLabel("معلومات مباشرة — بدون تغيير في الأتمتة")

    local dashStatus = DashboardTab:AddLabel("الحالة: جارٍ التحميل...")
    local dashStats = DashboardTab:AddLabel("الإحصائيات: جارٍ التحميل...")
    local dashSystems = DashboardTab:AddLabel("الأنظمة: جارٍ التحميل...")

    local function devilDashboardText()
        local ls = LP and LP:FindFirstChild("leaderstats")
        local strength = ls and ls:FindFirstChild("Strength")
        local rebirths = ls and ls:FindFirstChild("الريبر")
        local durability = LP and LP:FindFirstChild("Durability")

        local master = getgenv()._DEVIL_MasterMode == true and "ON" or "OFF"
        local farming = getgenv().Autoالتدريب == true and "ON" or "OFF"
        local boss = getgenv()._DEVIL_AutoBoss == true and "ON" or "OFF"
        local pets = getgenv().AutoHatchPet == true and "ON" or "OFF"

        return "MASTER: " .. master
            .. " | التدريب: " .. farming
            .. " | Boss: " .. boss
            .. " | Pets: " .. pets,
            "Strength: " .. tostring(strength and strength.Value or "?")
            .. " | الريبر: " .. tostring(rebirths and rebirths.Value or "?")
            .. " | Durability: " .. tostring(durability and durability.Value or "?"),
            "الوقت: " .. os.date("%H:%M:%S")
    end

    task.spawn(function()
        while DashboardTab and task.wait(0.75) do
            pcall(function()
                local a,b,c = devilDashboardText()
                dashStatus.Text = "Status: " .. a
                dashStats.Text = a and b or "الإحصائيات: ?"
                dashSystems.Text = c
            end)
        end
    end)

    PerformanceTab:AddLabel("أداء DEVIL")
    PerformanceTab:AddButton("🛑 إيقاف جميع أنظمة DEVIL", function()
        getgenv()._DEVIL_StopAll()
    end)
    PerformanceTab:AddButton("🧹 تنظيف اتصالات DEVIL", function()
        for key, connection in pairs(getgenv()._DEVIL_Connections or {}) do
            pcall(function()
                if connection and connection.Disconnect then connection:Disconnect() end
            end)
            getgenv()._DEVIL_Connections[key] = nil
        end
    end)

    PerformanceTab:AddLabel("أدوات للإيقاف الآمن وتنظيف الاتصالات")

    local cleanupConnections = {}
    getgenv()._DEVIL_Connections = cleanupConnections

    local function devilDisconnectAll()
        local count = 0
        for i, connection in pairs(cleanupConnections) do
            if connection then
                pcall(function()
                    if connection.Disconnect then
                        connection:Disconnect()
                    end
                end)
                cleanupConnections[i] = nil
                count = count + 1
            end
        end
        return count
    end
    getgenv()._DEVIL_DisconnectAll = devilDisconnectAll

    PerformanceTab:AddButton("إيقاف DEVIL الآمن", function()
        getgenv().Autoالتدريب = false
        getgenv().AutoالتدريبNoPacks = false
        getgenv().autoFarm = false
        getgenv()._DEVIL_MasterMode = false
        getgenv()._DEVIL_MasterBossWanted = false
        getgenv()._DEVIL_MasterRebirthWanted = false
        getgenv()._DEVIL_AutoRebirth = false
        getgenv().AutoHatchPet = false
        if type(getgenv()._DEVIL_SetAutoBoss) == "function" then
            pcall(function() getgenv()._DEVIL_SetAutoBoss(false) end)
        end
        devilDisconnectAll()
    end)

    PerformanceTab:AddButton("تنظيف اتصالات DEVIL", function()
        devilDisconnectAll()
    end)

    PerformanceTab:AddLabel("سجل الاتصالات جاهز لأنظمة DEVIL المستقبلية.")
    PerformanceTab:AddLabel("الإيقاف الآمن يعطل أتمتة DEVIL بدون حذف الواجهة.")
end

------------------------------------------------------------------
-- DEVIL PHASE 5: SYSTEM GUARD / CONNECTION MANAGER
------------------------------------------------------------------
do
    local GuardTab = window:AddTab("DEVIL GUARD")
    getgenv()._DEVIL_SystemState = getgenv()._DEVIL_SystemState or {}
    getgenv()._DEVIL_Connections = getgenv()._DEVIL_Connections or {}

    local function registerConnection(name, connection)
        if not name or not connection then return connection end
        local old = getgenv()._DEVIL_Connections[name]
        if old and old ~= connection then
            pcall(function() old:Disconnect() end)
        end
        getgenv()._DEVIL_Connections[name] = connection
        return connection
    end

    local function disconnectConnection(name)
        local c = getgenv()._DEVIL_Connections[name]
        if c then pcall(function() if c.Disconnect then c:Disconnect() end end) end
        getgenv()._DEVIL_Connections[name] = nil
    end

    getgenv()._DEVIL_RegisterConnection = registerConnection
    getgenv()._DEVIL_DisconnectConnection = disconnectConnection

    local function setSystem(name, enabled)
        getgenv()._DEVIL_SystemState[name] = enabled == true
    end
    local function systemEnabled(name)
        return getgenv()._DEVIL_SystemState[name] == true
    end
    getgenv()._DEVIL_SetSystemState = setSystem
    getgenv()._DEVIL_IsSystemEnabled = systemEnabled

    GuardTab:AddLabel("حماية نظام DEVIL")
    GuardTab:AddLabel("يمنع تكرار تشغيل المهام والاتصالات.")

    GuardTab:AddButton("🛑 إيقاف طارئ", function()
        if type(getgenv()._DEVIL_StopAll) == "function" then
            pcall(getgenv()._DEVIL_StopAll)
        end
        getgenv()._DEVIL_SystemState = {}
        getgenv()._DEVIL_TaskLocks = {}
        GuardTab:AddLabel("الإيقاف الطارئ: تم إيقاف جميع أتمتة DEVIL")
    end)

    GuardTab:AddButton("🧹 فصل الاتصالات المسجلة", function()
        local removed = 0
        for name, c in pairs(getgenv()._DEVIL_Connections) do
            pcall(function() if c and c.Disconnect then c:Disconnect() end end)
            getgenv()._DEVIL_Connections[name] = nil
            removed += 1
        end
        GuardTab:AddLabel("تم فصل: " .. tostring(removed))
    end)

    local guardStatus = GuardTab:AddLabel("الأنظمة: جاهزة")
    task.spawn(function()
        while GuardTab and task.wait(0.75) do
            pcall(function()
                local active = 0
                for _, v in pairs(getgenv()._DEVIL_SystemState or {}) do
                    if v then active += 1 end
                end
                local locked = 0
                for _, v in pairs(getgenv()._DEVIL_TaskLocks or {}) do
                    if v then locked += 1 end
                end
                local conns = 0
                for _, v in pairs(getgenv()._DEVIL_Connections or {}) do
                    if v then conns += 1 end
                end
                guardStatus.Text = "Systems: " .. active .. " | Locks: " .. locked .. " | Connections: " .. conns
            end)
        end
    end)
end

------------------------------------------------------------------
-- DEVIL PHASE 7: دورة MASTER CONTROLLER
-- التدريب -> Boss -> Rebirth -> التدريب
-- Uses existing DEVIL systems; does not create new remotes.
------------------------------------------------------------------
do
    local CycleTab = window:AddTab("DEVIL CYCLE")

    getgenv()._DEVIL_MasterCycle = getgenv()._DEVIL_MasterCycle or {
        enabled = false,
        state = "IDLE",
        lastRebirth = -1,
        transition = 0,
        generation = 0,
    }

    local Cycle = getgenv()._DEVIL_MasterCycle
    local cycleGeneration = 0

    local StateLabel = CycleTab:AddLabel("الدورة: متوقفة")
    local DetailLabel = CycleTab:AddLabel("في انتظار MASTER...")

    CycleTab:AddSwitch("دورة MASTER", function(state)
        Cycle.enabled = state == true
        cycleGeneration += 1
        Cycle.generation = cycleGeneration

        if Cycle.enabled then
            Cycle.state = "FARMING"
            Cycle.transition = os.clock()
            getgenv()._DEVIL_MasterMode = true
            getgenv()._DEVIL_MasterState = true
            getgenv().Autoالتدريب = true
        else
            Cycle.state = "IDLE"
            Cycle.transition = os.clock()
            getgenv()._DEVIL_MasterState = false
        end
    end)

    CycleTab:AddSwitch("أولوية الزعيم", function(state)
        getgenv()._DEVIL_MasterBossWanted = state == true
    end)

    CycleTab:AddSwitch("ريبر تلقائي", function(state)
        getgenv()._DEVIL_MasterRebirthWanted = state == true
    end)

    local function getStats()
        local lp = game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer
        local ls = lp and lp:FindFirstChild("leaderstats")
        return ls and ls:FindFirstChild("Strength"), ls and ls:FindFirstChild("الريبر")
    end

    local function hasBossSystem()
        return type(getgenv()._DEVIL_SetAutoBoss) == "function"
            and getgenv()._DEVIL_AutoBoss ~= nil
    end

    local function setBoss(on)
        if hasBossSystem() then
            pcall(getgenv()._DEVIL_SetAutoBoss, on == true)
        end
    end

    task.spawn(function(myGeneration)
        while true do
            task.wait(0.25)

            if not Cycle.enabled or Cycle.generation ~= myGeneration then
                myGeneration = Cycle.generation
            end

            if not Cycle.enabled then
                Cycle.state = "IDLE"
            else
                local strength, rebirths = getStats()
                local currentالريبر = rebirths and tonumber(rebirths.Value) or 0
                local required = 10000 + (5000 * currentالريبر)

                if Cycle.lastRebirth >= 0 and currentالريبر > Cycle.lastRebirth then
                    Cycle.state = "FARMING"
                    Cycle.transition = os.clock()
                    getgenv().Autoالتدريب = true
                    if not getgenv()._DEVIL_MasterBossWanted then
                        setBoss(false)
                    end
                end

                Cycle.lastRebirth = currentالريبر

                if getgenv()._DEVIL_MasterBossWanted and hasBossSystem() then
                    if getgenv()._DEVIL_AutoBoss == true then
                        Cycle.state = "BOSS"
                    elseif Cycle.state == "BOSS" then
                        Cycle.state = "FARMING"
                    end
                end

                if Cycle.state == "FARMING" then
                    getgenv().Autoالتدريب = true

                    if getgenv()._DEVIL_MasterRebirthWanted
                        and strength
                        and strength.Value >= required then
                        Cycle.state = "REBIRTH"
                        Cycle.transition = os.clock()
                        getgenv().Autoالتدريب = false
                    end

                elseif Cycle.state == "REBIRTH" then
                    getgenv().Autoالتدريب = false
                    setBoss(false)

                    if getgenv()._DEVIL_MasterRebirthWanted and strength and rebirths then
                        if strength.Value >= required then
                            local before = rebirths.Value
                            local rEvents = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage"):FindFirstChild("rEvents")
                            local remote = rEvents and rEvents:FindFirstChild("rebirthRemote")

                            if remote then
                                pcall(function()
                                    remote:InvokeServer("rebirthRequest")
                                end)
                            end

                            if rebirths.Value > before then
                                Cycle.state = "FARMING"
                                Cycle.transition = os.clock()
                            end
                        else
                            Cycle.state = "FARMING"
                        end
                    else
                        Cycle.state = "FARMING"
                    end

                elseif Cycle.state == "BOSS" then
                    getgenv().Autoالتدريب = false
                    setBoss(true)
                end
            end

            pcall(function()
                local elapsed = math.max(0, os.clock() - (Cycle.transition or os.clock()))
                StateLabel.Text = "Cycle: " .. tostring(Cycle.state)
                DetailLabel.Text = string.format("State time: %.1fs | الريبر: %s", elapsed, tostring((getStats() and select(2, getStats()) and select(2, getStats()).Value) or "?"))
            end)
        end
    end, Cycle.generation)
end

------------------------------------------------------------------

------------------------------------------------------------------
-- DEVIL PHASE 8: RECOVERY WATCHDOG
-- Keeps existing DEVIL automation paused during respawn and restores it safely.
------------------------------------------------------------------
do
    local RecoveryTab = window:AddTab("DEVIL RECOVERY")
    getgenv()._DEVIL_Recovery = getgenv()._DEVIL_Recovery or {enabled=true, recovering=false, generation=0}
    local Recovery = getgenv()._DEVIL_Recovery

    RecoveryTab:AddLabel("مراقب استعادة DEVIL")
    local RecoveryStatus = RecoveryTab:AddLabel("الاستعادة: جاهزة")

    RecoveryTab:AddSwitch("استعادة تلقائية بعد إعادة الظهور", function(state)
        Recovery.enabled = state == true
    end)

    local function pauseAutomation()
        getgenv()._DEVIL_RecoveryWasالتدريب = getgenv().Autoالتدريب == true
        getgenv()._DEVIL_RecoveryWasNoPacks = getgenv().AutoالتدريبNoPacks == true
        getgenv()._DEVIL_RecoveryWasMaster = getgenv()._DEVIL_MasterMode == true
        getgenv().Autoالتدريب = false
        getgenv().AutoالتدريبNoPacks = false
        getgenv()._AutoRepFarmEnabled = false
    end

    local function resumeAutomation()
        if getgenv()._DEVIL_RecoveryWasالتدريب then
            getgenv().Autoالتدريب = true
        end
        if getgenv()._DEVIL_RecoveryWasNoPacks then
            getgenv().AutoالتدريبNoPacks = true
        end
        if getgenv()._DEVIL_RecoveryWasMaster then
            getgenv()._DEVIL_MasterMode = true
            getgenv()._DEVIL_MasterState = true
        end
    end

    local function handleCharacter(character)
        if not Recovery.enabled then return end
        Recovery.recovering = true
        Recovery.generation += 1
        local myGeneration = Recovery.generation
        pauseAutomation()
        RecoveryStatus.Text = "Recovery: Waiting for character"

        local humanoid = character:WaitForChild("Humanoid", 10)
        local root = character:WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart", 10)
        if not humanoid or not root or myGeneration ~= Recovery.generation then
            Recovery.recovering = false
            return
        end

        task.wait(1)
        if myGeneration ~= Recovery.generation then return end

        resumeAutomation()
        Recovery.recovering = false
        RecoveryStatus.Text = "Recovery: Restored"
        task.delay(2, function()
            if not Recovery.recovering then
                pcall(function() RecoveryStatus.Text = "الاستعادة: جاهزة" end)
            end
        end)
    end

    local lp = game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer
    if lp then
        if lp.Character then
            task.spawn(handleCharacter, lp.Character)
        end
        local connection = lp.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(character)
            task.spawn(handleCharacter, character)
        end)
        if type(getgenv()._DEVIL_RegisterConnection) == "function" then
            getgenv()._DEVIL_RegisterConnection("Recovery.CharacterAdded", connection)
        end
    end
end

------------------------------------------------------------------
-- DEVIL PHASE 9: RUNTIME HEALTH MONITOR
-- Detects stalled automation without creating duplicate workers.
------------------------------------------------------------------
do
    local HealthTab = window:AddTab("DEVIL HEALTH")
    local Health = getgenv()._DEVIL_Health or {
        enabled = true,
        generation = 0,
        lastStrength = nil,
        lastالريبر = nil,
        lastProgressAt = os.clock(),
        stalled = false,
        checks = 0,
    }
    getgenv()._DEVIL_Health = Health

    HealthTab:AddLabel("صحة تشغيل DEVIL")
    local HealthStatus = HealthTab:AddLabel("الصحة: جارٍ التهيئة...")
    local HealthDetail = HealthTab:AddLabel("التقدم: في انتظار الإحصائيات")

    HealthTab:AddSwitch("مراقبة الصحة تلقائيًا", function(state)
        Health.enabled = state == true
        if Health.enabled then
            Health.lastProgressAt = os.clock()
            Health.stalled = false
        end
    end)

    local function readHealthStats()
        local lp = game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer
        local ls = lp and lp:FindFirstChild("leaderstats")
        local strength = ls and ls:FindFirstChild("Strength")
        local rebirths = ls and ls:FindFirstChild("الريبر")
        return strength, rebirths
    end

    local function healthSnapshot()
        local strength, rebirths = readHealthStats()
        return strength and tonumber(strength.Value) or nil,
               rebirths and tonumber(rebirths.Value) or nil
    end

    task.spawn(function()
        while HealthTab and task.wait(2) do
            if not Health.enabled then
                pcall(function()
                    HealthStatus.Text = "Health: Disabled"
                end)
            else
                local strength, rebirths = healthSnapshot()
                Health.checks += 1

                if Health.lastStrength == nil then
                    Health.lastStrength = strength
                    Health.lastالريبر = rebirths
                    Health.lastProgressAt = os.clock()
                elseif strength ~= Health.lastStrength or rebirths ~= Health.lastالريبر then
                    Health.lastStrength = strength
                    Health.lastالريبر = rebirths
                    Health.lastProgressAt = os.clock()
                    Health.stalled = false
                end

                local stalledFor = os.clock() - Health.lastProgressAt
                local automationActive =
                    getgenv().Autoالتدريب == true
                    or getgenv().AutoالتدريبNoPacks == true
                    or getgenv()._DEVIL_MasterMode == true

                -- A health warning is intentionally non-destructive.
                -- It does not toggle remotes or start a second farming loop.
                Health.stalled = automationActive and stalledFor >= 15

                pcall(function()
                    HealthStatus.Text = Health.stalled and
                        "Health: STALLED (monitoring)" or
                        "Health: OK"
                    HealthDetail.Text = string.format(
                        "Strength: %s | الريبر: %s | No progress: %.1fs | Checks: %d",
                        tostring(strength or "?"),
                        tostring(rebirths or "?"),
                        stalledFor,
                        Health.checks
                    )
                end)
            end
        end
    end)
end

-- وضع DEVIL MASTER
------------------------------------------------------------------
do
    local MasterTab = window:AddTab("DEVIL MASTER")
    local masterRunning = false
    local masterAutoRebirth = false

    MasterTab:AddLabel("وضع DEVIL MASTER")
    MasterTab:AddLabel("تدريب → زعيم → ريبر → حيوانات")

    MasterTab:AddSwitch("MASTER", function(state)
        masterRunning = state == true
        getgenv()._DEVIL_MasterMode = masterRunning

        if masterRunning then
            -- بدء the main farming engine. Boss handling can temporarily pause it.
            getgenv().Autoالتدريب = true
            if getgenv()._DEVIL_AutoBoss ~= nil then
                getgenv()._DEVIL_MasterBossWanted = true
            end
        else
            getgenv().Autoالتدريب = false
            getgenv().AutoالتدريبNoPacks = false
            getgenv()._DEVIL_MasterBossWanted = false
            getgenv()._DEVIL_MasterRebirthWanted = false
            if type(getgenv()._DEVIL_SetAutoBoss) == "function" then
                getgenv()._DEVIL_SetAutoBoss(false)
            end
        end
    end)

    MasterTab:AddSwitch("ريبر MASTER تلقائي", function(state)
        masterAutoRebirth = state == true
        getgenv()._DEVIL_MasterRebirthWanted = masterAutoRebirth
    end)

    MasterTab:AddSwitch("أولوية زعيم MASTER", function(state)
        getgenv()._DEVIL_MasterBossWanted = state == true
    end)

    MasterTab:AddLabel("الحالة: DEVIL MASTER يتحكم في دورة الأتمتة الرئيسية.")

    local StatusLabel = MasterTab:AddLabel("الحالة: جارٍ التهيئة...")

    local function __DEVIL_StatusText()
        local lp = game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer
        local ls = lp and lp:FindFirstChild("leaderstats")
        local strength = ls and ls:FindFirstChild("Strength")
        local rebirths = ls and ls:FindFirstChild("الريبر")

        local farming = getgenv().Autoالتدريب == true and "ON" or "OFF"
        local boss = getgenv()._DEVIL_AutoBoss == true and "ON" or "OFF"
        local master = getgenv()._DEVIL_MasterMode == true and "ON" or "OFF"
        local autoRb = getgenv()._DEVIL_MasterRebirthWanted == true and "ON" or "OFF"
        local pet = getgenv().AutoHatchPet == true and "ON" or "OFF"

        return "MASTER: " .. master
            .. " | التدريب: " .. farming
            .. " | Boss: " .. boss
            .. " | Rebirth: " .. autoRb
            .. " | Pets: " .. pet
            .. " | Strength: " .. tostring(strength and strength.Value or "?")
            .. " | الريبر: " .. tostring(rebirths and rebirths.Value or "?")
    end

    task.spawn(function()
        while MasterTab and task.wait(0.5) do
            pcall(function()
                StatusLabel.Text = __DEVIL_StatusText()
            end)
        end
    end)

    task.spawn(function()
        while true do
            if masterRunning and not getgenv()._DEVIL_MasterControllerV3 then
                local lp = game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer
                local ls = lp and lp:FindFirstChild("leaderstats")
                local strength = ls and ls:FindFirstChild("Strength")
                local rebirths = ls and ls:FindFirstChild("الريبر")
                local rEvents = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage"):FindFirstChild("rEvents")

                -- Boss priority is intentionally delegated to the existing Boss system.
                -- It already saves/restores the التدريب state around Boss encounters.
                if getgenv()._DEVIL_MasterBossWanted and getgenv()._DEVIL_AutoBoss == false
                    and type(getgenv()._DEVIL_SetAutoBoss) == "function" then
                    getgenv()._DEVIL_SetAutoBoss(true)
                end

                -- Rebirth only when explicitly enabled in Master Mode.
                if getgenv()._DEVIL_MasterRebirthWanted and strength and rebirths and rEvents then
                    local ok, required = pcall(function()
                        return 10000 + (5000 * rebirths.Value)
                    end)
                    if ok and strength.Value >= required then
                        pcall(function()
                            rEvents.rebirthRemote:InvokeServer("rebirthRequest")
                        end)
                    end
                end
            end
            task.wait(0.25)
        end
    end)
end

------------------------------------------------------------------
-- DEVIL HUB V1.01 — MAIN PARITY EXTENSIONS
------------------------------------------------------------------
do
    local MainParity = window:AddTab("Main Extras")
    local LP = game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer
    local RS = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
    local WS = game:GetService("Workspace")
    local RunService = game:GetService("RunService")

    -- Anti AFK is enabled automatically at script startup.
    MainParity:AddButton("مضاد AFK [تشغيل]", function()
        setupAntiAFK()
    end)

    -- ريبر تلقائي
    MainParity:AddLabel("الريبر")
    local rebirthTarget = 0
    local rebirthRunning = false
    getgenv()._DEVIL_AutoRebirth = false
    MainParity:AddTextBox("تحديد هدف الريبر", function(v)
        local n = tonumber(v)
        if n then rebirthTarget = math.max(0, math.floor(n)) end
    end, {clear=false})
    MainParity:AddSwitch("ريبر تلقائي", function(on)
        rebirthRunning = on
        getgenv()._DEVIL_AutoRebirth = on
        if not on then return end
        task.spawn(function()
            while rebirthRunning and rebirthTarget > 0 do
                local ls = LP:FindFirstChild("leaderstats")
                local rb = ls and ls:FindFirstChild("الريبر")
                if rb and rb.Value >= rebirthTarget then break end
                pcall(function() RS.rEvents.rebirthRemote:InvokeServer("rebirthRequest") end)
                task.wait(0.08)
            end
            rebirthRunning = false
        end)
    end)

    -- حجم تلقائي 1
    local sizeOne = false
    MainParity:AddSwitch("حجم تلقائي 1", function(on) sizeOne = on end)
    task.spawn(function()
        while true do
            if sizeOne then
                pcall(function()
                    local r = RS.rEvents:FindFirstChild("changeSpeedSizeRemote")
                    if r then r:InvokeServer("changeSize", 1) end
                end)
                pcall(function()
                    local r = RS.rEvents:FindFirstChild("changeSpeedSize")
                    if r then r:InvokeServer("changeSize", 1) end
                end)
            end
            task.wait(0.05)
        end
    end)

    -- الملك تلقائي
    local kingOn = false
    local kingCF = CFrame.new(-8665.4, 17.21, -5792.9)
    MainParity:AddSwitch("الملك تلقائي", function(on) kingOn = on end)
    task.spawn(function()
        while true do
            if kingOn then
                local c = LP.Character
                local hrp = c and c:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                local hum = c and c:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                if hrp and hum and hum.Health > 0 and (hrp.Position - kingCF.Position).Magnitude > 5 then
                    pcall(function() hrp.CFrame = kingCF end)
                end
            end
            task.wait(0.1)
        end
    end)

    -- Squat / Lift machine farming
    local machinesFolder = WS:FindFirstChild("machinesFolder")
    local function interactPart(machine)
        if not machine then return nil end
        for _, d in ipairs(machine:GetDescendants()) do
            if d:IsA("BasePart") and string.lower(d.Name) == "interactseat" then return d end
        end
    end
    local function machineList(keyword, excluded)
        local out = {}
        if not machinesFolder then return out end
        for _, m in ipairs(machinesFolder:GetChildren()) do
            local n = string.lower(m.Name)
            if string.find(n, keyword, 1, true) and n ~= excluded then table.insert(out, m) end
        end
        table.sort(out, function(a,b) return a.Name < b.Name end)
        return out
    end
    local squatChoices, liftChoices = {}, {}
    for _, m in ipairs(machineList("squat", "squat rack")) do squatChoices[m.Name] = m end
    for _, m in ipairs(machineList("lift", "deadlift")) do liftChoices[m.Name] = m end

    local function addMachineFarm(label, choices)
        local selected, running = nil, false

        -- سجل مشترك حتى يستطيع Auto Boss حفظ واستعادة هذه الأنظمة
        -- رغم أن المتغيرات الأصلية داخل هذه الدالة محلية.
        local stateKey = (label == "Select Squat" and "Squat") or (label == "Select Lift" and "Lift") or label
        getgenv()._DEVIL_MachineFarmState = getgenv()._DEVIL_MachineFarmState or {}
        getgenv()._DEVIL_MachineFarmState[stateKey] = {
            selected = nil
        }

        local state = getgenv()._DEVIL_MachineFarmState[stateKey]
        state.selected = selected
        state.running = false
        state.toggle = nil

        local dd = MainParity:AddDropdown(label, function(v)
            selected = choices[v]
            state.selected = selected
        end)
        for name in pairs(choices) do dd:Add(name) end
        local machineToggle = MainParity:AddSwitch("Auto " .. label:gsub("Select ", ""), function(on)
            running = on
            state.running = on
            state.selected = selected
            if not on then return end
            task.spawn(function()
                while running do
                    local c = LP.Character
                    local hrp = c and c:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                    local hum = c and c:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                    local seat = interactPart(selected)
                    if hrp and hum and hum.Health > 0 and seat then
                        pcall(function()
                            hrp.CFrame = seat.CFrame * CFrame.new(0,3,0)
                            hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
                            hrp.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
                        end)
                        task.wait(0.35)
                        pcall(function()
                            game:GetService("VirtualInputManager"):SendKeyEvent(true, Enum.KeyCode.E, false, game)
                            task.wait(0.08)
                            game:GetService("VirtualInputManager"):SendKeyEvent(false, Enum.KeyCode.E, false, game)
                        end)
                        task.wait(0.35)
                        pcall(function() LP.muscleEvent:FireServer("rep") end)
                    end
                    task.wait(0.05)
                end
            end)
        end)
        state.toggle = machineToggle
    end
    addMachineFarm("Select Squat", squatChoices)
    addMachineFarm("Select Lift", liftChoices)

    -- اختيار الصخرة + صخرة تلقائية (separate from DEVIL's existing Rock tab)
    MainParity:AddLabel("صخرة تلقائية")
    local rockData = {
        ["Tiny Rock - 0 Dura"] = 0, ["Large Rock - 100 Dura"] = 100,
        ["Punching Rock - 10 Dura"] = 10, ["Golden Rock - 5k Dura"] = 5000,
        ["Frost Rock - 150k Dura"] = 150000, ["Mythical Rock - 400k Dura"] = 400000,
        ["Eternal Rock - 750k Dura"] = 750000, ["Legend Rock - 1m Dura"] = 1000000,
        ["Muscle King Rock - 5m Dura"] = 5000000, ["Jungle Rock - 10m Dura"] = 10000000,
        ["الصخرة الصناعية - تحمل 25م"] = 25000000
    }
    local selectedRock
    getgenv()._DEVIL_MainParityRockState = getgenv()._DEVIL_MainParityRockState or {
        running = false,
        selected = nil
    }
    local rockDD = MainParity:AddDropdown("اختيار الصخرة", function(v)
        selectedRock = v
        getgenv()._DEVIL_MainParityRockState.selected = v
    end)
    for name in pairs(rockData) do rockDD:Add(name) end
    local rockRunning = false
    MainParity:AddSwitch("صخرة تلقائية", function(on)
        rockRunning = on
        getgenv()._DEVIL_MainParityRockState.running = on
        if not on then return end
        task.spawn(function()
            while rockRunning do
                local req = selectedRock and rockData[selectedRock]
                local dura = LP:FindFirstChild("Durability")
                local c = LP.Character
                local lh, rh = c and c:FindFirstChild("LeftHand"), c and c:FindFirstChild("RightHand")
                if req and dura and dura.Value >= req and lh and rh and machinesFolder then
                    for _, v in ipairs(machinesFolder:GetDescendants()) do
                        if not rockRunning then break end
                        if v.Name == "neededDurability" and v.Value == req and v.Parent:FindFirstChild("Rock") then
                            local rock = v.Parent.Rock
                            pcall(function()
                                firetouchinterest(rock, rh, 0); firetouchinterest(rock, rh, 1)
                                firetouchinterest(rock, lh, 0); firetouchinterest(rock, lh, 1)
                                LP.muscleEvent:FireServer("punch", "leftHand")
                                LP.muscleEvent:FireServer("punch", "rightHand")
                            end)
                        end
                    end
                end
                task.wait(0.12)
            end
        end)
    end)

    -- تدريب قوة أفضل presets
    MainParity:AddLabel("تدريب قوة أفضل")
    local presets = {
        ["Pushup + Industrial Rock"] = {tool="Pushups", rock="Industrial Rock"},
        ["Pushup + Jungle Rock"] = {tool="Pushups", rock="صخرة الغابة القديمة"},
        ["Pushup + Muscle King Rock"] = {tool="Pushups", rock="Muscle King Mountain"},
        ["Pushup + Legends Rock"] = {tool="Pushups", rock="Rock Of Legends"}
    }
    local presetStates = {}
    local function startPreset(name, cfg)
        task.spawn(function()
            while presetStates[name] do
                local c = LP.Character
                local hum = c and c:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                if c and hum then
                    local tool = c:FindFirstChild(cfg.tool) or LP.Backpack:FindFirstChild(cfg.tool)
                    if tool and tool.Parent == LP.Backpack then pcall(function() hum:EquipTool(tool) end) end
                    pcall(function() LP.muscleEvent:FireServer("rep") end)
                    if machinesFolder then
                        local m = machinesFolder:FindFirstChild(cfg.rock)
                        if m and m:FindFirstChild("Rock") and c:FindFirstChild("LeftHand") then
                            pcall(function()
                                firetouchinterest(m.Rock, c.LeftHand, 0); firetouchinterest(m.Rock, c.LeftHand, 1)
                            end)
                        end
                    end
                end
                task.wait(0.05)
            end
        end)
    end
    for name, cfg in pairs(presets) do
        MainParity:AddSwitch(name, function(on)
            presetStates[name] = on
            if on then startPreset(name, cfg) end
        end)
    end

    -- Best stat pets
    local function equipBest(priority)
        local pf = LP:FindFirstChild("petsFolder")
        local ev = RS.rEvents:FindFirstChild("equipPetEvent")
        if not pf or not ev then return end
        for _, folder in ipairs(pf:GetChildren()) do
            if folder:IsA("Folder") then
                for _, pet in ipairs(folder:GetChildren()) do pcall(function() ev:FireServer("unequipPet", pet) end) end
            end
        end
        task.wait(0.2)
        local unique = pf:FindFirstChild("Unique")
        if not unique then return end
        local count = 0
        for _, wanted in ipairs(priority) do
            for _, pet in ipairs(unique:GetChildren()) do
                if pet.Name == wanted and count < 9 then
                    pcall(function() ev:FireServer("equipPet", pet) end)
                    count += 1
                    task.wait(0.08)
                end
            end
        end
    end
    MainParity:AddButton("تجهيز أفضل ضرر", function()
        equipBest({"Wild Wizard", "Chaos Sorcerer", "Mighty Monster", "Small Fry"})
    end)
    MainParity:AddButton("تجهيز أفضل صحة", function()
        equipBest({"Mighty Monster", "Small Fry", "Wild Wizard", "Chaos Sorcerer"})
    end)

    -- لون الحلقة for the existing DEVIL ring
    MainParity:AddLabel("لون الحلقة")
    local ringColors = {
        Silence=Color3.fromRGB(138,0,0), Red=Color3.fromRGB(255,0,0), Orange=Color3.fromRGB(255,128,0),
        Yellow=Color3.fromRGB(255,230,0), Lime=Color3.fromRGB(128,255,0), Green=Color3.fromRGB(0,200,70),
        Cyan=Color3.fromRGB(0,220,255), Blue=Color3.fromRGB(0,100,255), Purple=Color3.fromRGB(145,70,255),
        Pink=Color3.fromRGB(255,80,180), White=Color3.fromRGB(255,255,255), Black=Color3.fromRGB(0,0,0)
    }
    local colorDD = MainParity:AddDropdown("لون الحلقة", function(name)
        local c = ringColors[name]
        if c then
            local r = WS:FindFirstChild("DEVIL_KillAuraRing") or WS:FindFirstChild("SilenceKillAuraRing")
            if r and r:IsA("BasePart") then r.Color = c end
            local globalRing = _G.DEVIL_KillAuraRing
            if globalRing and globalRing:IsA("BasePart") then globalRing.Color = c end
        end
    end)
    for name in pairs(ringColors) do colorDD:Add(name) end
end

------------------------------------------------------------------
-- DEVIL HUB V1.01 — EXTRA FEATURES / MAIN PARITY
------------------------------------------------------------------
do
    local Extra = window:AddTab("Extras")
    local LP = game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer
    local RS = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
    local UIS = game:GetService("UserInputService")
    local Lighting = game:GetService("Lighting")
    local RunService = game:GetService("RunService")

    Extra:AddLabel("الحركة / المظهر")

    Extra:AddTextBox("سرعة المشي", function(v)
        local n = tonumber(v)
        if n and LP.Character then
            local h = LP.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            if h then h.سرعة المشي = math.clamp(n, 0, 250) end
        end
    end, {clear=false})

    Extra:AddTextBox("مجال الرؤية", function(v)
        local n = tonumber(v)
        if n and workspace.CurrentCamera then
            workspace.CurrentCamera.FieldOfView = math.clamp(n, 1, 120)
        end
    end, {clear=false})

    local infiniteJump = false
    Extra:AddSwitch("قفز لا نهائي", function(v) infiniteJump = v end)
    UIS.JumpRequest:Connect(function()
        if infiniteJump and LP.Character then
            local h = LP.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            if h then h:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Jumping) end
        end
    end)

    local hidePets = false
    local function updatePets()
        local c = LP.Character
        if not c then return end
        for _,d in ipairs(c:GetDescendants()) do
            if d:IsA("BasePart") and d.Name:lower():find("pet") then
                d.LocalTransparencyModifier = hidePets and 1 or 0
            end
        end
    end
    Extra:AddSwitch("إخفاء الحيوانات", function(v) hidePets=v; updatePets() end)
    RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function() if hidePets then updatePets() end end)

    local hidePopups = false
    Extra:AddSwitch("إخفاء النوافذ المنبثقة", function(v) hidePopups=v end)
    RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
        if not hidePopups then return end
        local pg = LP:FindFirstChildOfClass("PlayerGui")
        if not pg then return end
        for _,d in ipairs(pg:GetDescendants()) do
            if (d:IsA("Frame") or d:IsA("TextLabel") or d:IsA("ImageLabel")) and d.Name:lower():find("popup") then
                d.Visible = false
            end
        end
    end)

    local walkWater = false
    Extra:AddSwitch("المشي على الماء", function(v) walkWater=v end)
    RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
        if not walkWater then return end
        for _,d in ipairs(workspace:GetDescendants()) do
            if d:IsA("BasePart") and d.Name:lower():find("water") then
                d.CanCollide = true
            end
        end
    end)

    Extra:AddDropdown("تغيير الوقت", function(v)
        Lighting.Clockالوقت = (v == "Night") and 0 or 9
    end):Add("Day")
    -- The library dropdown returns its object, so add Night separately.
    local timeDrop = Extra:AddDropdown("الوقت", function(v) Lighting.Clockالوقت = (v=="Night") and 0 or 9 end)
    timeDrop:Add("Day")
    timeDrop:Add("Night")

    Extra:AddLabel("التدريب")

    local exerciseRunning = false
    local selectedExercise = "Pushups"
    getgenv()._DEVIL_AutoExerciseState = getgenv()._DEVIL_AutoExerciseState or {
        running = false,
        selected = selectedExercise
    }
    local exDrop = Extra:AddDropdown("اختيار التمرين", function(v)
        selectedExercise=v
        getgenv()._DEVIL_AutoExerciseState.selected=v
    end)
    for _,v in ipairs({"Weight","Pushups","Situps","Handstands"}) do exDrop:Add(v) end
    Extra:AddSwitch("تمرين تلقائي", function(v)
        exerciseRunning=v
        getgenv()._DEVIL_AutoExerciseState.running=v
        if v then
            task.spawn(function()
                while exerciseRunning do
                    local char=LP.Character
                    local tool=(LP.Backpack and LP.Backpack:FindFirstChild(selectedExercise)) or (char and char:FindFirstChild(selectedExercise))
                    if tool and char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid") then
                        char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid"):EquipTool(tool)
                    end
                    local me=LP:FindFirstChild("muscleEvent")
                    if me then pcall(function() me:FireServer("rep") end) end
                    task.wait(0.08)
                end
            end)
        end
    end)

    Extra:AddButton("مسح القائمة البيضاء", function()
        if type(playerWhitelist)=="table" then table.clear(playerWhitelist) end
    end)
    Extra:AddButton("مسح قائمة القتل", function()
        if type(targetPlayerNames)=="table" then table.clear(targetPlayerNames) end
    end)

    Extra:AddLabel("عجلة الحظ")
    local wheelRunning=false
    local wheelRemote
    pcall(function() wheelRemote=RS.rEvents:FindFirstChild("openFortuneWheelRemote") end)
    Extra:AddSwitch("تدوير عجلة الحظ", function(v)
        wheelRunning=v
        if v and wheelRemote then
            task.spawn(function()
                local chances
                pcall(function() chances=RS.shared.catalogs["fortuneWheelChances"]["عجلة الحظ"] end)
                while wheelRunning do
                    pcall(function() wheelRemote:InvokeServer("openFortuneWheel", chances) end)
                    task.wait(1)
                end
            end)
        end
    end)

    Extra:AddLabel("حلقة هالة القتل")
    local ringOn=false
    local ringPart
    local ringRange=20
    Extra:AddTextBox("مدى الحلقة", function(v)
        local n=tonumber(v)
        if n then ringRange=math.clamp(n,1,140); if ringPart then ringPart.Size=Vector3.new(.2,ringRange*2,ringRange*2) end end
    end,{clear=false})
    Extra:AddSwitch("إظهار الحلقة", function(v)
        ringOn=v
        if ringOn then
            if ringPart then ringPart:Destroy() end
            ringPart=Instance.new("Part")
            ringPart.Name="DEVIL_KillAuraRing"
            ringPart.Shape=Enum.PartType.Cylinder
            ringPart.Anchored=true
            ringPart.CanCollide=false
            ringPart.CanTouch=false
            ringPart.CanQuery=false
            ringPart.Transparency=.6
            ringPart.Material=Enum.Material.Neon
            ringPart.Size=Vector3.new(.2,ringRange*2,ringRange*2)
            ringPart.Parent=workspace
        elseif ringPart then ringPart:Destroy(); ringPart=nil end
    end)
    RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
        if ringOn and ringPart and LP.Character and LP.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
            ringPart.CFrame=LP.Character.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame*CFrame.Angles(0,0,math.rad(90))
        end
    end)
end

-- DEVIL HUB CONFIG SYSTEM
-- Phase 4: persistent runtime configuration for DEVIL settings.
do
    local ConfigTab = window:AddTab("⚙️ Config")
    local CONFIG_FILE = "DEVIL_HUB_Config.json"
    getgenv()._DEVIL_ConfigAutoLoad = getgenv()._DEVIL_ConfigAutoLoad ~= false
    local HttpService = game:GetService("HttpService")

    local function copyList(list)
        local out = {}
        if type(list) == "table" then
            for i, v in ipairs(list) do out[i] = v end
        end
        return out
    end

    local function listToSet(list)
        local out = {}
        if type(list) == "table" then
            for _, v in ipairs(list) do out[v] = true end
        end
        return out
    end

    local function getConfig()
        return {
            Version = 4,
            AutoExercise = getgenv()._DEVIL_AutoExerciseState or {running=false, selected="Pushups"},

            -- Core automation
            MasterMode = getgenv()._DEVIL_MasterMode == true,
            MasterBossWanted = getgenv()._DEVIL_MasterBossWanted == true,
            MasterRebirthWanted = getgenv()._DEVIL_MasterRebirthWanted == true,
            AutoBoss = getgenv()._DEVIL_AutoBoss == true,
            Autoالتدريب = getgenv().Autoالتدريب == true,
            AutoالتدريبNoPacks = getgenv().AutoالتدريبNoPacks == true,
            AdaptivePingTraining = DEVIL_PING_TRAINING.enabled == true,
            AutoHatchPet = _G.AutoHatchPet == true or getgenv().AutoHatchPet == true,
            AutoHatchAura = _G.AutoHatchAura == true,
            AutoEvolve = _G.DEVILAutoEvolve == true,
            AutoTrade = tradeRunning == true,

            -- Boss settings
            BossContinue = getgenv()._DEVIL_BossContinue == true,
            BossAutoPunch = getgenv()._DEVIL_AutoPunch == true,
            BossAutoChest = getgenv()._DEVIL_AutoChest == true,
            BossSaveBeforeRebirth = getgenv()._DEVIL_SaveBeforeRebirth == true,

            -- Inventory settings
            PetBuyAmount = tonumber(PetShopData.BuyAmount) or 1,
            AuraBuyAmount = tonumber(AuraData.BuyAmount) or 1,
            EvolveAmount = tonumber(EvolveData.Amount) or 1,
            SelectedPets = copyList(PetShopData.SelectedList),
            SelectedAuras = copyList(AuraData.SelectedList),
            SelectedEvolvePets = copyList(EvolveData.SelectedList),
            SelectedTradePlayers = copyList(TradeData.SelectedPlayerList),
            SelectedTradePets = copyList(TradeData.SelectedPetList),

            -- Extra / UI
            HidePets = hidePets == true,
            HidePopups = hidePopups == true,
            WalkWater = walkWater == true,
            InfiniteJump = infiniteJump == true,
            Wheel = wheelRunning == true,
            Ring = ringOn == true,
            RingRange = tonumber(ringRange) or 20,
            الإشعارات = getgenv()._DEVIL_الإشعارات ~= false,
            RespawnRecovery = getgenv()._DEVIL_RespawnRecovery ~= false,
            DebugMode = getgenv()._DEVIL_DebugMode == true,
            ConfigAutoLoad = getgenv()._DEVIL_ConfigAutoLoad == true,
        }
    end

    local function saveConfig(silent)
        if not writefile then
            if not silent and type(getgenv()._DEVIL_Notify) == "function" then
                getgenv()._DEVIL_Notify("DEVIL HUB", "writefile is not available", 3)
            end
            return false
        end
        local ok, encoded = pcall(function()
            return HttpService:JSONEncode(getConfig())
        end)
        if not ok then return false end
        local wrote = pcall(writefile, CONFIG_FILE, encoded)
        if wrote and not silent and type(getgenv()._DEVIL_Notify) == "function" then
            getgenv()._DEVIL_Notify("DEVIL HUB", "Config saved", 2)
        end
        return wrote
    end

    local function loadConfig()
        if not (readfile and isfile and isfile(CONFIG_FILE)) then return nil end
        local ok, raw = pcall(readfile, CONFIG_FILE)
        if not ok then return nil end
        local decodedOK, cfg = pcall(function() return HttpService:JSONDecode(raw) end)
        if decodedOK and type(cfg) == "table" then return cfg end
        return nil
    end

    local function applySelected(data, list)
        table.clear(data.Selected)
        table.clear(data.SelectedList)
        if type(list) ~= "table" then return end
        for _, name in ipairs(list) do
            if type(name) == "string" and name ~= "" then
                data.Selected[name] = true
                table.insert(data.SelectedList, name)
            end
        end
    end

    local function refreshSelectionLabels()
        if petLabel then petLabel.Text = "Selected Pets: " .. (#PetShopData.SelectedList > 0 and table.concat(PetShopData.SelectedList, ", ") or "None") end
        if auraLabel then auraLabel.Text = "Selected الهالات: " .. (#AuraData.SelectedList > 0 and table.concat(AuraData.SelectedList, ", ") or "None") end
        if evolveLabel then evolveLabel.Text = "Selected Pets: " .. (#EvolveData.SelectedList > 0 and table.concat(EvolveData.SelectedList, ", ") or "None") end
        if tradePlayerLabel then tradePlayerLabel.Text = "Players: " .. (#TradeData.SelectedPlayerList > 0 and table.concat(TradeData.SelectedPlayerList, ", ") or "None") end
        if tradePetLabel then tradePetLabel.Text = "Pets: " .. (#TradeData.SelectedPetList > 0 and table.concat(TradeData.SelectedPetList, ", ") or "None") end
    end

    local function applyConfig(cfg)
        if type(cfg) ~= "table" then return false end

        if type(cfg.AutoExercise) == "table" then
            getgenv()._DEVIL_AutoExerciseState = cfg.AutoExercise
            if cfg.AutoExercise.selected then selectedExercise = cfg.AutoExercise.selected end
            exerciseRunning = cfg.AutoExercise.running == true
        end

        PetShopData.BuyAmount = math.clamp(math.floor(tonumber(cfg.PetBuyAmount) or PetShopData.BuyAmount or 1), 1, 999)
        AuraData.BuyAmount = math.clamp(math.floor(tonumber(cfg.AuraBuyAmount) or AuraData.BuyAmount or 1), 1, 999)
        EvolveData.Amount = math.clamp(math.floor(tonumber(cfg.EvolveAmount) or EvolveData.Amount or 1), 1, 999)
        applySelected(PetShopData, cfg.SelectedPets)
        applySelected(AuraData, cfg.SelectedAuras)
        applySelected(EvolveData, cfg.SelectedEvolvePets)
        applySelected(TradeData, cfg.SelectedTradePlayers)
        -- TradeData uses separate SelectedPets storage, so restore it explicitly.
        table.clear(TradeData.SelectedPets)
        table.clear(TradeData.SelectedPetList)
        if type(cfg.SelectedTradePets) == "table" then
            for _, name in ipairs(cfg.SelectedTradePets) do
                if type(name) == "string" and name ~= "" then
                    TradeData.SelectedPets[name] = true
                    table.insert(TradeData.SelectedPetList, name)
                end
            end
        end

        hidePets = cfg.HidePets == true
        hidePopups = cfg.HidePopups == true
        walkWater = cfg.WalkWater == true
        infiniteJump = cfg.InfiniteJump == true
        wheelRunning = cfg.Wheel == true
        ringOn = cfg.Ring == true
        if tonumber(cfg.RingRange) then ringRange = math.clamp(tonumber(cfg.RingRange), 1, 140) end

        getgenv()._DEVIL_الإشعارات = cfg.الإشعارات ~= false
        getgenv()._DEVIL_RespawnRecovery = cfg.RespawnRecovery ~= false
        getgenv()._DEVIL_DebugMode = cfg.DebugMode == true
        getgenv()._DEVIL_ConfigAutoLoad = cfg.ConfigAutoLoad ~= false

        getgenv()._DEVIL_MasterMode = cfg.MasterMode == true
        getgenv()._DEVIL_MasterState = getgenv()._DEVIL_MasterMode
        getgenv()._DEVIL_MasterBossWanted = cfg.MasterBossWanted == true
        getgenv()._DEVIL_MasterRebirthWanted = cfg.MasterRebirthWanted == true
        getgenv()._DEVIL_AutoBoss = cfg.AutoBoss == true
        getgenv().Autoالتدريب = cfg.Autoالتدريب == true
        getgenv().AutoالتدريبNoPacks = cfg.AutoالتدريبNoPacks == true
        if DEVIL_PING_TRAINING then DEVIL_PING_TRAINING.enabled = cfg.AdaptivePingTraining ~= false end
        _G.AutoHatchPet = cfg.AutoHatchPet == true
        getgenv().AutoHatchPet = _G.AutoHatchPet
        _G.AutoHatchAura = cfg.AutoHatchAura == true
        _G.DEVILAutoEvolve = cfg.AutoEvolve == true

        refreshSelectionLabels()
        if type(getgenv()._DEVIL_Notify) == "function" then
            getgenv()._DEVIL_Notify("DEVIL HUB", "Config loaded", 2)
        end
        return true
    end

    ConfigTab:AddLabel("إعدادات DEVIL HUB v4")
    ConfigTab:AddButton("💾 حفظ الإعدادات", function() saveConfig(false) end)
    ConfigTab:AddButton("📂 تحميل الإعدادات", function()
        local cfg = loadConfig()
        if not cfg then
            if type(getgenv()._DEVIL_Notify) == "function" then getgenv()._DEVIL_Notify("DEVIL HUB", "No valid config found", 3) end
            return
        end
        applyConfig(cfg)
    end)

    ConfigTab:AddButton("💾 حفظ الحالة الحالية", function() saveConfig(false) end)
    ConfigTab:AddButton("🗑️ إعادة ضبط الإعدادات", function()
        if isfile and isfile(CONFIG_FILE) and delfile then
            pcall(delfile, CONFIG_FILE)
            if type(getgenv()._DEVIL_Notify) == "function" then getgenv()._DEVIL_Notify("DEVIL HUB", "Config reset", 2) end
        end
    end)

    ConfigTab:AddSwitch("حفظ الإعدادات تلقائيًا", function(state)
        getgenv()._DEVIL_ConfigAutoSave = state == true
        if state then
            task.spawn(function()
                while getgenv()._DEVIL_ConfigAutoSave do
                    saveConfig(true)
                    task.wait(10)
                end
            end)
        end
    end)

    ConfigTab:AddSwitch("تحميل الإعدادات تلقائيًا", function(state)
        getgenv()._DEVIL_ConfigAutoLoad = state == true
    end)

    ConfigTab:AddLabel("ملف الإعدادات: " .. CONFIG_FILE)
    ConfigTab:AddLabel("يحفظ إعدادات MASTER والزعيم والتدريب والحيوانات والهالات والتطوير والتداول والواجهة.")

    -- Optional automatic restore once, after the UI/runtime has finished creating its systems.
    task.delay(1.5, function()
        if getgenv()._DEVIL_ConfigAutoLoad then
            local cfg = loadConfig()
            if cfg then applyConfig(cfg) end
        end
    end)
end

------------------------------------------------------------------
-- DEVIL PHASE 2: NOTIFICATIONS + RESPAWN RECOVERY + DEBUG + MONITOR
------------------------------------------------------------------
do
    local Phase2Tab = window:AddTab("أدوات DEVIL")
    local Players = game:GetService("Players")
    local RunService = game:GetService("RunService")
    local LP = Players.LocalPlayer

    getgenv()._DEVIL_الإشعارات = getgenv()._DEVIL_الإشعارات ~= false
    getgenv()._DEVIL_DebugMode = getgenv()._DEVIL_DebugMode == true
    getgenv()._DEVIL_RespawnRecovery = getgenv()._DEVIL_RespawnRecovery ~= false
    getgenv()._DEVIL_Notify = getgenv()._DEVIL_Notify or function(title, message, duration)
        if not getgenv()._DEVIL_الإشعارات then return end
        pcall(function()
            game:GetService("بدءerGui"):SetCore("SendNotification", {
                Title = tostring(title or "DEVIL HUB"),
                Text = tostring(message or ""),
                Duration = tonumber(duration) or 3
            })
        end)
    end

    getgenv()._DEVIL_DebugLog = getgenv()._DEVIL_DebugLog or function(message)
        if not getgenv()._DEVIL_DebugMode then return end
        pcall(function()
            warn("[DEVIL DEBUG] " .. tostring(message))
        end)
    end

    local function notify(title, message, duration)
        getgenv()._DEVIL_Notify(title, message, duration)
    end

    Phase2Tab:AddLabel("أدوات DEVIL")
    Phase2Tab:AddLabel("الإشعارات / الاستعادة / التصحيح / الأداء")

    Phase2Tab:AddSwitch("الإشعارات", function(state)
        getgenv()._DEVIL_الإشعارات = state == true
        if state then notify("DEVIL HUB", "الإشعارات enabled", 2) end
    end)

    Phase2Tab:AddSwitch("استعادة تلقائية بعد إعادة الظهور", function(state)
        getgenv()._DEVIL_RespawnRecovery = state == true
    end)

    Phase2Tab:AddSwitch("وضع التصحيح", function(state)
        getgenv()._DEVIL_DebugMode = state == true
        if state then
            warn("[DEVIL DEBUG] Debug mode enabled")
        end
    end)

    local perfLabel = Phase2Tab:AddLabel("الأداء: جارٍ الجمع...")
    local taskLabel = Phase2Tab:AddLabel("الأقفال النشطة: 0")
    local connLabel = Phase2Tab:AddLabel("الاتصالات المسجلة: 0")

    local function countTable(t)
        local n = 0
        if type(t) == "table" then
            for _ in pairs(t) do n += 1 end
        end
        return n
    end

    local function updatePerformance()
        local locks = countTable(getgenv()._DEVIL_TaskLocks)
        local conns = countTable(getgenv()._DEVIL_Connections)
        local heartbeat = RunService.Heartbeat
        local dt = heartbeat and heartbeat:Wait() or 0
        local fps = dt > 0 and math.floor(1 / dt + 0.5) or 0
        taskLabel.Text = "Active locks: " .. tostring(locks)
        connLabel.Text = "Registered connections: " .. tostring(conns)
        perfLabel.Text = "Performance: ~" .. tostring(fps) .. " FPS"
    end

    task.spawn(function()
        while Phase2Tab and task.wait(1) do
            pcall(updatePerformance)
        end
    end)

    -- Recovery keeps the user's desired runtime states across character respawns.
    local recoveryConnection
    local function applyRecovery()
        if not getgenv()._DEVIL_RespawnRecovery then return end
        task.delay(1, function()
            if not LP or not LP.Parent then return end
            if getgenv()._DEVIL_MasterMode then
                getgenv().Autoالتدريب = true
            end
            if getgenv()._DEVIL_MasterBossWanted and type(getgenv()._DEVIL_SetAutoBoss) == "function" then
                pcall(getgenv()._DEVIL_SetAutoBoss, true)
            end
            if getgenv()._DEVIL_AutoBoss then
                getgenv()._DEVIL_AutoBoss = true
            end
            if getgenv().AutoHatchPet then
                _G.AutoHatchPet = true
            end
            getgenv()._DEVIL_DebugLog("Respawn recovery applied")
        end)
    end

    if getgenv()._DEVIL_Connections.RespawnRecovery then
        pcall(function() getgenv()._DEVIL_Connections.RespawnRecovery:Disconnect() end)
    end
    recoveryConnection = LP.CharacterAdded:Connect(function()
        if getgenv()._DEVIL_RespawnRecovery then
            notify("DEVIL HUB", "Character respawned — recovering active systems", 3)
            applyRecovery()
        end
    end)
    getgenv()._DEVIL_Connections.RespawnRecovery = recoveryConnection

    -- Lightweight lifecycle notifications. These only observe state; they do not alter automation.
    local lastالريبر
    local lastBossState = false
    task.spawn(function()
        while Phase2Tab and task.wait(0.5) do
            pcall(function()
                local ls = LP:FindFirstChild("leaderstats")
                local rb = ls and ls:FindFirstChild("الريبر")
                if rb then
                    local value = tonumber(rb.Value)
                    if lastالريبر ~= nil and value and value > lastالريبر then
                        notify("DEVIL HUB", "Rebirth complete: " .. tostring(value), 3)
                    end
                    lastالريبر = value
                end

                local bossState = getgenv()._DEVIL_AutoBoss == true
                if bossState and not lastBossState then
                    notify("DEVIL HUB", "Auto Boss activated", 2)
                end
                lastBossState = bossState
            end)
        end
    end)

    Phase2Tab:AddButton("🧪 اختبار الإشعار", function()
        notify("DEVIL HUB", "Notification system is working", 3)
    end)

    Phase2Tab:AddButton("🐞 لقطة التصحيح", function()
        local locks = countTable(getgenv()._DEVIL_TaskLocks)
        local conns = countTable(getgenv()._DEVIL_Connections)
        local master = getgenv()._DEVIL_MasterMode == true
        local farming = getgenv().Autoالتدريب == true
        local boss = getgenv()._DEVIL_AutoBoss == true
        warn("[DEVIL DEBUG] Master=" .. tostring(master)
            .. " التدريب=" .. tostring(farming)
            .. " Boss=" .. tostring(boss)
            .. " Locks=" .. tostring(locks)
            .. " Connections=" .. tostring(conns))
        notify("DEVIL DEBUG", "Snapshot written to executor console", 3)
    end)
end


------------------------------------------------------------------
-- DEVIL PHASE 3: MASTER STATE MACHINE
------------------------------------------------------------------
do
    getgenv()._DEVIL_MasterControllerV3 = true
    getgenv()._DEVIL_MasterPhase = getgenv()._DEVIL_MasterPhase or "IDLE"
    getgenv()._DEVIL_MasterLastRebirth = getgenv()._DEVIL_MasterLastRebirth
    getgenv()._DEVIL_MasterBusyUntil = 0

    local Players = game:GetService("Players")
    local ReplicatedStorage = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
    local CollectionService = game:GetService("CollectionService")
    local Workspace = game:GetService("Workspace")
    local LP = Players.LocalPlayer

    local function masterNotify(message)
        if type(getgenv()._DEVIL_Notify) == "function" then
            getgenv()._DEVIL_Notify("DEVIL MASTER", message, 2)
        end
    end

    local function bossExists()
        for _, obj in ipairs(CollectionService:GetTagged("BossEventBoss")) do
            if obj and obj:IsDescendantOf(Workspace) then
                return true
            end
        end
        return false
    end

    local function getStats()
        local ls = LP and LP:FindFirstChild("leaderstats")
        return ls and ls:FindFirstChild("Strength"), ls and ls:FindFirstChild("الريبر")
    end

    local function getRebirthRequirement(rebirthValue)
        local n = tonumber(rebirthValue) or 0
        return 10000 + (5000 * n)
    end

    local function doRebirth()
        local rEvents = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("rEvents")
        local remote = rEvents and rEvents:FindFirstChild("rebirthRemote")
        if not remote then return false end
        local ok = pcall(function()
            remote:InvokeServer("rebirthRequest")
        end)
        return ok
    end

    local function masterStopالتدريب()
        getgenv().Autoالتدريب = false
        getgenv().AutoالتدريبNoPacks = false
    end

    local function masterبدءالتدريب()
        getgenv().Autoالتدريب = true
        getgenv().AutoالتدريبNoPacks = false
    end

    local function masterStopBoss()
        if type(getgenv()._DEVIL_SetAutoBoss) == "function" then
            pcall(getgenv()._DEVIL_SetAutoBoss, false)
        else
            getgenv()._DEVIL_AutoBoss = false
        end
    end

    local function masterبدءBoss()
        if type(getgenv()._DEVIL_SetAutoBoss) == "function" then
            pcall(getgenv()._DEVIL_SetAutoBoss, true)
        else
            getgenv()._DEVIL_AutoBoss = true
        end
    end

    local function masterSetPhase(phase)
        if getgenv()._DEVIL_MasterPhase ~= phase then
            getgenv()._DEVIL_MasterPhase = phase
            if getgenv()._DEVIL_DebugMode then
                warn("[DEVIL MASTER] Phase -> " .. tostring(phase))
            end
            if phase == "BOSS" then
                masterNotify("Boss priority active")
            elseif phase == "REBIRTH" then
                masterNotify("Rebirth threshold reached")
            elseif phase == "FARMING" then
                masterNotify("Returned to farming")
            end
        end
    end

    task.spawn(function()
        while true do
            task.wait(0.25)

            if getgenv()._DEVIL_MasterMode ~= true then
                masterSetPhase("IDLE")
                continue
            end

            local now = os.clock()
            if now < (getgenv()._DEVIL_MasterBusyUntil or 0) then
                continue
            end

            local strength, rebirths = getStats()
            local strengthValue = strength and tonumber(strength.Value) or 0
            local rebirthValue = rebirths and tonumber(rebirths.Value) or 0
            local wantsBoss = getgenv()._DEVIL_MasterBossWanted == true
            local wantsRebirth = getgenv()._DEVIL_MasterRebirthWanted == true
            local bossActive = bossExists()

            if wantsBoss and bossActive then
                masterSetPhase("BOSS")
                -- The existing Boss system owns combat, dodge and chest handling.
                masterStopالتدريب()
                masterبدءBoss()
                getgenv()._DEVIL_MasterBusyUntil = now + 0.75
                continue
            end

            if getgenv()._DEVIL_AutoBoss == true and wantsBoss then
                -- Keep Boss armed while still allowing normal farming until a boss actually appears.
                masterSetPhase("FARMING")
                masterبدءالتدريب()
                getgenv()._DEVIL_MasterBusyUntil = now + 0.75
                continue
            end

            if wantsRebirth and strengthValue >= getRebirthRequirement(rebirthValue) then
                masterSetPhase("REBIRTH")
                masterStopBoss()
                masterStopالتدريب()
                local before = rebirthValue
                if doRebirth() then
                    getgenv()._DEVIL_MasterBusyUntil = now + 1.0
                    task.delay(0.6, function()
                        local _, latestالريبر = getStats()
                        local after = latestالريبر and tonumber(latestالريبر.Value) or before
                        if after > before then
                            masterNotify("Rebirth complete: " .. tostring(after))
                        end
                    end)
                end
                continue
            end

            masterSetPhase("FARMING")
            masterStopBoss()
            masterبدءالتدريب()
            getgenv()._DEVIL_MasterBusyUntil = now + 0.75
        end
    end)
end

local Credits = window:AddTab("الحقوق")
